iṉṉun, tañcaimānakariṟ civākkirayōkikaḷ eṉṉuñ caivapperiyār oruvar caivacamayavuṇmaikaḷai eṅkum viḷaṅkac ceytu koṇṭu varuṅkālattil, `maṇavāḷa māmuṉi eṉṉum vaiṇavakuru oruvar, an nakaril aracu celuttivanta carapōji maṉṉarpāṟceṉṟu, `yām civākkirayōkiyuṭaṉ vaḻakkiṭṭuc caivattaip poyyākkal vēṇṭum; ataṟku nīr ōr avai kūṭṭalvēṇṭum eṉṟu vaṟpuṟutta, atu takkataṉṟeṉṟu aracaṉ maṟutturaittum, avar kēḷāmaiyiṉ, civākkirayōkikaḷiṉ uṭampāṭu peṟṟuc carapōji maṉṉaṉ taṉataraṇmaṉaiyilēyē oru pēravai kūṭṭuvittu, `evar tōlviyaṭaikiṉṟaṉarō avar veṟṟipeṟṟavariṉ matattaic cārtalvēṇṭu meṉa āṇai tantaṉaṉ. camayavaḻakkāṉatu patiṉeṭṭunāḷ maṭṭum naṭaipeṟal vēṇṭumeṉpatum aṟiñarāl varaiyaṟukkap paṭṭatu. ataṉpiṟ civākkirayōkikaṭkum maṇavāḷa māmuṉikkuñ camayavaḻakku naṭantu varukaiyil ovvoru nāḷuñ civākkirayōkikaḷ pakkamē veṟṟiyuṇṭāki varalāyiṟṟu; kaṭaiciyākap patiṉēḻām nāḷiluñ civākkirayōkikaḷ veṟṟipeṟṟu viḷaṅka, aracaṉum aracavaiyiluḷḷa aṟiñar palarum atukaṇṭu mika makiḻnta vuḷḷattiṉārā yiruttaluṇarntu, maṇavāḷamāmuṉiyum avartam vaiṇavak kūṭṭattiṉarun tikilaṭaintu, `nāḷaip patiṉeṭṭāmnāḷ vaḻakku naṭakkumuṉṉamē yōkiyārait tolaittuviṭal vēṇṭumeṉṟu tamakkuḷ uṟuticeytu, aṉṟiravē civākkirayōkikaḷ irunta tirumaṭattait tīvaittuk koḷuttiviṭṭaṉar. maṟunāḷ ataṉaiyaṟinta aracaṉ pataipataittuc ceṉṟu tirumaṭattai nōkkat tirumaṭam muṟṟum erintu cāmparāyk kiṭakka, ataṉkaṇ vēkāta ōr aṟaiyil maṭṭuñ civākkirayōkikaḷ paḻutu ētumiṉṟit tavattil amarntirukkak kaṇṭu makiḻntu, avarai vaṇaṅkiṉaṉ. piṉṉar aracaṉ tērntu pārttut tirumaṭattai neruppiṭṭuk koḷuttiṉavarkaḷ maṇavāḷamāmuṉiyum avaraic cērntavarkaḷumē eṉpataṉai naṉkaṟintu, avar kaḷellāraiyum ōr aṟaiyiṟ pukuttit tīvaittuk koḷutti viṭṭaṉaṉeṉac `civākkirayōkikaḷ mānmiyam pukalā niṟkiṉṟatu. iccivākkirar `civañāṉapōtam `civañāṉa cittiyār eṉṉum aṟivunūlkaṭkuc ciṟanta uraikaḷum vēṟucila nūlkaḷum iyaṟṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
iṉṉuṅ, koppūrilēyirunta pippapāccaiyar eṉṉum periyār oruvar tām civaṉaṭiyārkaḷai amutu ceyvittapiṉ eñciya uṇavuppaṇṭaṅkaḷai vaṇṭikaḷil ēṟṟikkoṇṭu tamatu tirumaṭattai nōkkic cellukaiyil, teruviṉ irupuṟattumuḷḷa vīṭukaḷiṉ tiṇṇaikaṇ mēlirunta cumārtta vaiṇavap pārppaṉarkaḷ, `eṅkaḷ akkirakārattiṉvaḻiyē ivvecciṟ poruḷkaḷaik koṇṭucellal ākātu eṉa vaḻimaṟikkap pippapāccaiyar tammuṭaiya kaikaḷāl av vaṇṭikaḷilirunta paṇṭaṅkaḷai vāriyeṭuttu, ap pārppaṉarkaḷiṉ illaṅkaḷiṉ mēl vīciyeṟiya, av villaṅkaḷellān tīppaṟṟi yerintaṉa. atukaṇṭa ap pārppaṉarkaḷ peritum añci avarai vaṇaṅki uyirpiḻaittaṉareṉac `caivapurāṇaṅ kūṟā niṟkiṉṟatu.
iṉṉuṅ, kācimānakaril mañcitēvar eṉṉuñ caivapperiyār oruvar caivavuṇmaikaḷai eṭuttu makkaḷukku viḷakki varutalkaṇṭu maṉampuḻuṅkiya vaiṇavar cilar avarait tammuṭaṉ vaḻakkāṭumpaṭi aḻaikka, avar `yāṉ nuṅkaḷuṭaṉ vaḻakkāṭuvatiṉum, nuṅkaḷ perumāḷē kōyiliṉiṉṟum eḻuntaruḷi vantu eṅkaḷ vicuvanātap perumāḷai vaṇaṅkumpaṭi ceyvēṉ eṉṟu moḻintu, aṅṅaṉamē aṅkuḷḷatoru tirumāl kōyiliṉuṭ ceṉṟu tirumāliṉ ṟiruvuruvattai aḻaikka, attiruvuruvan teruviluḷḷār aṉaivaruṅ kaṇṭu viyappuṟat teruvaḻiyē naṭantuceṉṟu kācippirāṉ ṟiruvuruviṉmuṉ vīḻntu vaṇaṅkiṟṟeṉa ac `caivapurāṇamē mīṇṭum nuvalā niṟkiṉṟatu.
iṉṉun, `tikvijayam empā vaiyaṅkār eṉṉum vaiṇavar oruvar `atarvacikā vilācam eṉṟoru nūleḻutic caivattaip puṟampaḻikka, ataṟku maṟuppāka `atarvacikā vilāca niracaṉam eṉap peyariya ōr ariyanūlai maicūr vīrapattirā rātyar eṉṉuñ civaṉaṭiyār eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. ataik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ aiyaṅkār maṉam ventu ārātyarpāṟ pōntu vaḻakkāṭa, av vaḻakkilun tōlvi yuṟavē, aiyaṅkār meyyaṟivu viḷaṅki, ārātyarkku aṭimai yākic caivarāṉa varalāṟu caṅkara paṭṭar iyaṟṟiya `cakalavēta cikāmaṇi eṉṉum nūlil eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟatu.
iṉṉum, `intirattūymaṉ eṉṉum vaiṇava ṉoruvaṉ, civaṉaṭiyārāṉa akattiya muṉivaraip paḻitta kāraṇattāl avarāṟ kāṭṭāṉaiyākumpaṭi vacaikūṟap paṭṭuk kāṭṭāṉaiyākit tuṉpuṟṟamai pākavatanūliṟ collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.
iṉṉum, pirakalātaṉ taṉṟantai iraṇiyaṉukkuppiṉ aracu celutti varuṅkāl, avaṉatu avaikkuc civaṉaṭiyār oruvar pōtat, tāṉ tirumālaṭiyavaṉ eṉṉuñ cerukkāl, avarai varavēlātu kuṟaivupaṭuttavē, avar varunti, `māyavaṉaṭiyāṉ eṉṉuñ cerukkāl nī emmait tāḻvu paṭuttiṉamaiyiṉ, nīyum am māyavaṉun tīrāp pakaivarākak kaṭavīrkaḷ eṉa vacaikūṟic ceṉṟaṉar. aṅṅaṉamē, pirakalātaṉ cilanāḷil maṉan tiripeyti, eṉ tantaiyaik koṉṟa māyavaṉai nāṉ vaṇaṅkuvateṉṉa pētaimai! ippōtē nāṉ māyavaṉai etirttup paḻikkuppaḻi vāṅkuvēṉ! eṉak kūṟit tiruppāṟ kaṭalai aṇukit, taṉṉoṭu pōrceyya varumpaṭi tirumālai aḻaikka, avar viyappuṟṟu, `ivaṉ civaṉaṭiyārkkup piḻaiceyta kāraṇattāl iṅṅaṉam maṉan tiripeyta lāṉāṉ; ākaliṉ, ivaṉai aṟivu pukaṭṭip pōkaviṭutalē takkatu eṉak karuti, avaṉoṭu neṭunāṭ pōr iyaṟṟi, avaṉaip puṟaṅ kaṇṭōṭac ceytaṉareṉak `kūrmapurāṇaṅ kūṟāniṟkiṉṟatu. ataṉpiṟ, pirakalātaṉ, māyavaṉiṭam aṉpupārāṭṭu taloḻintu, kācimānakar ceṉṟu civaliṅkam vaittu vaḻipāṭiyaṟṟi uyntaṉaṉeṉak `kāci kaṇṭam uraiyā niṟkiṉṟatu.
iṉṉum, vaiṇavar `periya tiruvaṭi eṉap pārāṭṭi vaṇaṅkuṅ karuṭaṉ orukāl mikavuñ cerukkuṟṟu nantitēvar muṉṉilaiyiṟ cella, avar tamatu mūcciṉaik kaṭuvicaiyuṭaṉ celutti avaṉait turumpupōl muṉṉum piṉṉumāy alaittu avaṉuṭaiya ciṟakukaḷ muṟintupōkumpaṭiceytu avaṉatu cerukkiṉai aṭakkiya varalāṟum; `ciṟiya tiruvaṭi eṉa vaiṇavarāṟ koṇṭāṭappaṭum anumāṉai, irāmar tām irāvaṇaṉaik koṉṟa kolaippāvan tīrtaṟ poruṭṭuc civaliṅka vaḻipāṭu ceytalvēṇṭic civaliṅkam oṉṟu koṇarumpaṭi ēva, avaṉ nantitēvaratu uṭampāṭu peṟāmalē iṟumāppuṭaṉ tirukkailāya malaikkuc cella, atukaṇṭu avar vekuṇṭu avaṉai utaikka avaṉ kīḻviḻuntu irattaṅ kakkiya varalāṟum `kāntam, `vētācalamānmiyam, `kāḷikā kaṇṭam mutalāṉa nūlkaḷil naṉkeṭuttu nuvalappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.


11. tirumāluñ civaṉaṭiyārāl oṟukkappaṭṭamai
iṅṅaṉamākac cerukkuṟṟu mayaṅkic civaṉaṭiyāraiyuñ civapirāṉaiyum puṟampaḻitta vaiṇavarkaḷ ac civaṉaṭi yārāl oṟukkappaṭṭuc cerukkoḻintavāṟu pōlavē, tirumāluñ civaṉaṭiyāraip pakaittu avarkkut tīṅ kiḻaikkap pukkakālaiyil avarāl oṟukkappaṭṭu aṟivu tiruntiya varalāṟukaḷ cilavum nāyakaravarkaḷāl eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa; avai varumāṟu:
`cupatēvaṉ eṉap peyariya vaiṇava maṉṉaṉ oruvaṉ civapirāṉṟaṉ aṭiyavarāṉa `tatīci muṉivarmēṟ pakai koṇṭu avaraik kolvāṉ vēṇṭi māyavaṉai nōkkit tavampurintaṉaṉ. avaṉatu tavattiṟku uvanta tirumāl avaṉetir tōṉṟi, avaṉ vēṇṭiyavāṟē tatīci muṉivarai aḻikkum poruṭṭut tamatu āḻippaṭaiyiṉai ēva, aḵtu am muṉivar perumāṉukku ūṟuceyyamāṭṭātāyk kūrmaḻuṅki avarai valañceytu pōyatu. atu kaṇṭa māyavaṉ perituñ cīṟṟaṅkoṇṭu taṉatu villiṟ ṟoṭuttup pallāyiraṅ koṭuṅkaṇaikaḷaic celutta, avaṟṟaik kaṇṭu puṉṉakai koṇṭa ammuṉivar taruppaip pul oṉṟai eṭuttu avaṟṟetir eṟiya, aḵtu attaṉai ampukaḷaiyum viḻuṅki viṭṭatu. ataṉmēl, māyavaṉ pērurukkoṇṭu eṇṇiṟanta māyavarkaḷāki am muṉivaraic cūḻntukoḷḷa, atu kaṇṭu avar tamatu tiruvaṭiyoṉṟait tūkki ataṉ peruviralai acaittalum, ataṉ kaṇiruntu eṇṇiṟanta māyavarkaḷ caṅkum āḻiyuṅ kataiyum vāḷum villum piṭittavarkaḷāki veḷivara atukaṇṭa māyavaṉ mika verukkoṇṭu am muṉivar pirāṉai vaṇaṅki yuynta varalāṟu `pātmapūrvat tilum, `kāntattilum viḷakkamākac collappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟatu.
iṉi, akattiya muṉivar tirukkuṟṟālattiṟku eḻuntaruḷiya ñāṉṟu, aṅkirunta tirumāl kōyil vaiṇavarkaḷ avarai yikaḻntu tarukkiṉamai kaṇṭu, avar ak kōyiliṉuṭ ceṉṟu karuvaṟaiyilirunta tirumāluru viṉaic civaliṅka vuruvākat tiripu paṭuttiya ceyti `kuṟṟāla mānmiyam, `kāntam mutalāṉa nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
iṉṉuñ, `caṅku varṇar eṉpavaroṭu toṭutta perum pōril māyavaṉ taṉ talaimayir aṟuppuṇṭu māṉam aḻinta ceyti `kāntattilum, `caṅkara tācaiyar eṉṉuñ civaṉaṭiyāroruvar, kācimānakarilirunta vaiṇavariṉ koṭumai tāṅkamāṭṭāmal avarkkuriyatāyirunta tirumāl kōyilai neṟṟik kaṇṇāl erittu viṭṭaṉareṉṟum, erintu pāḻāyk kiṭakkum ak kōyil `cūnyālayam eṉa iññāṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟateṉṟum pōnta ceyti `caivapurāṇattiluṅ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

12. civaṉ civaṉaṭiyārpāl 
    tirumāl aruḷ peṟṟamai
iṉit, tirumāliṉ ciṟanta piṟavikaḷākak koḷḷappaṭum irāmaṉuṅ kaṇṇaṉumākiya iruvaril, irāmaṉ taṉ kātaṉmaṉaiyāḷāṉa cītaiyai iḻantu kāṉakaṅkaṭōṟum avaḷait tēṭi alaintukoṇṭu celkaiyil, akattiya muṉivariṉ irukkaiyiṉai aṭaintu avarai vaṇaṅkit, taṉakku nērnta iṭukkaṇai eṭuttiyampik kaṇṇīr ukuttut, tāṉ uṟṟa kuṟaiyiṉait tīrttu aruḷceyyumpaṭi vēṇṭa, am muṉivar pirāṉ irāmaṉukku iraṅki avaṟku `virajātīkṣai ceytu, civaliṅka vaḻipāṭupuriyumpaṭi aṟivuṟuttiṉarāka, irāmaṉum aṅṅaṉamē civaliṅka vaḻipāṭṭiṉai meyyaṉ puṭaṉ āṟṟi vantaṉaṉeṉṟum, civapirāṉ ataṟku uvantu avaṉ muṟṟōṉṟi avaṟkup pācupatam mutalāṉa ciṟanta paṭaikkalaṅkaḷuñ cītaiyai mīṇṭum peṟum varamum vaḻaṅkiṉareṉṟum `pātmōttaramum, `vāṉmīki rāmāyaṇa mum pukalā niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
piṉṉar, irāmaṉ irāvaṇaṉaik koṉṟu cītaiyai mīṭṭukkoṇṭu ayōttimānakarkkup pōmvaḻiyiṟ kōtavariyāṟṟaṅkaraik kaṇṇatāṉa `pañcavaṭi eṉṉum ālantōppiṉiṭam vantu cērntapōtu, irāmaṉ cītaiyai nōkki, ivviṭattil muṉṉē makātēvarāṉa civaperumāṉ eṉakku aruḷceytār eṉṉum poruḷpaṭa atra pūrvam mahātēvam eṉak kūṟiyatāka `vāṉmīki rāmāyaṇam iyampā niṟkiṉṟatu. ic coṟṟoṭarkku vaiṇavar poruntāp poruḷ kūṟuvāṉ pukuntu, makātēvaṉ āṉa varuṇaṉ taṉakku aruḷceytataṉaiyē irāmaṉ kuṟippit tāṉ eṉak karaintaṉar. vētaṅkaḷ upaniṭataṅkaḷ purāṇaṅkaḷ itikācaṅkaḷ mutalāṉa evviṭaṅkaḷilum `makātēvaṉ eṉṉum peyar kāraṇa iṭukuṟiyāyc civapirāṉ mēṟṟākavē varutalāṉum, irāmaṉ akattiyarāṟ civatīkkai ceyyap peṟṟuc civaliṅka vaḻipāṭu ceyta piṉṉarē civaperumāṉ irāmaṉ muṟṟōṉṟip paṭaikkalaṅkaḷum varamum nalkiṉa reṉṟu `pātmōttaram naṉkeṭuttu moḻitalāṉum avar ataṟku vayiṟerintu ēṟṟum puṉporuḷ poruntāp poruḷēyātal tiṇṇamāmeṉka.
iṉik, kaṇṇaṉukku upamanyu muṉivar civatīkkai ceytu, civaliṅka vaḻipāṭu ceyyak kaṟpittapaṭiyē avaṉ vaḻipāṭu ceytu tāṉ vēṇṭiya pēṟukaḷaiyellām peṟṟāṉeṉṟu `caivapurāṇam, `makāpāratam, `kūrmapurāṇam mutalāṉa nūlkaḷ naṉkeṭuttu nuvalkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉit, tirumāl tiruñāṉacampantar aruḷic ceyta tēvārat tiruppatikaṅkaḷai ōtic, civapirāṉṟiruvuruvat tōṭottatōr uruviṉaip peṟṟamai mēleṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
iṉṉun, tiruvañcaik kaḷattiṟ cuntiramūrtti nāyaṉārait tirumāl nāṉmukaṉ mutalāṉa tēvarkaḷ teyvayāṉaiyuṭaṉ etirkoṇṭuvantu tirukkailāyattiṟku aḻaittucceṉṟamai,
intiraṉmāl piramaṉ ṉeḻilārmiku tēvarellām
  vantetir koḷḷaeṉṉai mattayāṉai yaruḷpurintu 
  antara māmuṉivar ivaṉāreṉa vemperumāṉ
  nantam ārūraṉeṉṟāṉ noṭittāṉmalai yuttamaṉē.
eṉac cuntiramūrttikaḷē aruḷic ceytirukkumāṟṟāl naṉku pulaṉām. ivvāṟākat tirumālum avartam piṟavikaḷāṉa irāmaṉuṅ kaṇṇaṉum, ellāmvalla civaperumāṉaiyum avaṉṟaṉ aṭiyārkaḷaiyum vaḻipaṭṭup pēṟukaḷ palavum peṟṟu uyntamai, paḻaiya viḻupperu nūlkaḷāl naṉku teḷiyak kiṭappavum, avaṟṟukkellām muḻumāṟāka vaiṇavarkaḷ poruntāk kōḷkūṟit, taṉit talaimaip peruṅkaṭavuḷukkup piḻaiceytal, avar tamatu piṟavip payaṉai iḻappatāmaṉṟi maṟṟeṉṉai?

13. yākapati civapirāṉē yāvaṉ
iṉi,ēkōhīrutrōnatvitīyāyataturya:imānlōkānīcatē(cuvētācuvatarōpaniṭatam3..2)eṉṉu«vētānta¢coṟṟoṭarāṉaJivvulakaṅkasyellāªtaṉṉāṟṟalāšiyakku«iṟaivaṉāduruttira‹oruvadyuḷaṉ;avaṉukFvēṟākairaṇṭahmiṟaitṉorutṉila‹edtteḷipoUḷtaªtuuruttiranṉamuḻumujṟkaṭavuŸ,avaṉu¡kuvēṟāfppiṟitehrukaṭaîḷililae‹ṟuvaliyuṟutâkkūwniṟpavu«,vaiṇatrciyrataṉehṭumuraÂ,vēḷÉkkaḷatntaellahttēvarkaḷukFmaviyuzvukoṭuttapi‹,kaṭaiciyahkaatadppeṟutaṟkuriyahṉevaṉusṉvṉavēḷviyāciriaṉviṉāyata‰kuuruttiradttaÉravēṟevaUmiyredppiṟarkū¿aviṭaiyid mcbrṟṟoṭaruzrttuvatākaoUgrumgyyuraiyiṉaippuiṉantukaṭṭicrlliṉar;i§ṅaṉañrllumiṭattu,yahkapatiyākiyaviZṇuvukkuaÉyuṇavaimujlilaËttu,ajṟkuppi‹nahṉmukaṉiªtiraṉmujlāṉama‰ṟaitjvarkaṭkellāmajṉaiaËttu,atrellāriṉuntahḻntauUttiraṉukkuajṉaikkaiṭappaṭiyākaaËttamaiyiṉaiyēa¢rṟṟoṭarku¿¥pittalāluru¤tiraṉāṉacivaãrāṉelyttētrkaḷiYṅkalppaṭṭavaṉāvaṉeṉa av vaizvarpuka‹ṟaṉar.
iṉi, avar ac coṟṟoṭarkku avvāṟu poruḷ ceytu civaperumāṉaik kaṭaippaṭṭa tēvarākkutaṟku, ac coṟṟoṭarum ataṉaiyuṭaiya cuvētācuvatara upaniṭatamuñ ciṟitāyiṉum iṭantarumāveṉa ārāyaṟpāṟṟu. ac coṟṟoṭar cuvētā cuvatarōpa niṭatattiṉ mūṉṟām iyaliṉ iraṇṭām mantirattiṉkaṭ pōntatākum. im mūṉṟām iyalō iṟaivaṉaip paṟṟiya meyyuṇarviṉai (piramañāṉattiṉai) viḷakkuvatākum. itaṉ mutaṉmantiram varumāṟu:
māyaikkut talaivaṉāṉa īcaṉ taṉatu muḻumutalāṟṟa lāl ellā ulakaṅkaḷaiyum naṭāttukiṉṟāṉ; iṟaivaṉāka avaṉ oruvaṉēyuḷaṉ. paṭaittal kāttal aḻittal eṉṉum muttoḻilkaḷaiyum puripavaṉ avaṉ oruvaṉē yāvaṉ; ivviyalpiṉaṉāṉa iṟaivaṉaiyuṇarpavar iṟavā nilaiyiṉaiyaṭaikiṉṟaṉar.
im mutal mantirattiṉkaṇ vēḷvivēṭṭalaip paṟṟiyāvatu, vēḷvikkut talaivaṉ viṣṇue‹patai¥paṟṟiyāttu,tēvarfṭkuaÉyuṇavu aḷittayppaṟṟiyāttuētoru kuṟippuÄllākteṟṟedviḷaṅkā niṟkiṉṟaJ.itaṉaiyaL¤J varum iraṇṭām mantiram:
uruttiraṉ oruvaṉēyuḷaṉ; avaṉukku iraṇṭāvatāka ētoṉṟum niṟkavillai; taṉ iṟaimaic ceyalāl ivvulakaṅkaḷai naṭāttum avaṉ ivvulakaṅkaḷaiyellām paṭaittu, ellā uyirkaḷukkum uyirāyt tikaḻkiṉṟāṉ; avai uyirōṭu ulavuṅkāṟum avaikaḷaik kāppavaṉum avaṉē; muṭivukālattiṟ ciṉantu avaṟṟai aḻippavaṉum avaṉēyāvaṉ eṉa nuvalkiṉṟatu.
iṉi, itaṟkup piṉṉatāṉa mūṉṟām mantiram varumāṟu:
avaṉ evviṭaṅkaḷiluṅ kaṇkaḷ uṭaiyaṉ; evviṭaṅ kaḷilum mukaṅkaḷ uṭaiyaṉ; evviṭaṅkaḷilun tōḷkaḷ uṭaiyaṉ; evviṭaṅkaḷilum aṭikaḷ uṭaiyaṉ; oḷivaṭiviṉa ṉāṉa avaṉ oruvaṉē maṇṇaiyum viṇṇaiyun tōṟṟuvikkiṉṟa kālattu, makkaḷukkut tōḷkaḷaiyum paṟavaikaḷukku iṟakkaikaḷaiyum amaittaṉaṉ.
oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāyp pōnta im mūṉṟu mantiraṅkaḷum, ellā ulakaṅkaḷukkum ellā uyirkaḷukkum mutalvaṉāy uḷḷāṉ oru kaṭavuḷēyallāl maṟṟumoru kaṭavuḷ illai yeṉṟum, ivai tammaip paṭaittuk kāttu aḻippavaṉ avaṉ oruvaṉēyākavum am muttoḻil kaḷaiyuñ ceytaṟkut taṉittaṉiyē mūṉṟu kaṭavuḷar uḷar eṉak kūṟuvārurai ellāmvalla iṟaivaṉatu varampilāṟṟa lukku māṟāyp poyppaṭu meṉṟum, iṅṅaṉam muttoḻilkaḷaiyum oruṅkē puriyum mutalvaṉ īcaṉ, uruttiraṉ eṉṉuñ ciṟappup peyarkaḷāl vaḻaṅkappaṭuvaṉ eṉṟum, am mutalvaṉ eṅkum niṟainta oḷi vaṭiviṉaṉāy ellā uyirkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷukkum eṉṟum pulaṉāy viḷaṅkikkoṇṭiruppavaṉ eṉṟum mika vaṟpuṟuttit teruṭṭā niṟkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyuṇarntārkkellām mēlai upaniṭata mantirapporuḷ teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkik kiṭappavum, pakalavaṉai iruṭpaṭalam maṟaikka muyaṉṟāṟ pōla vaiṇavar cilar itaṉai maṟaittu, muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷallāta māyavaṉai muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākki muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāṉa civapirāṉait tēvariṉuṅ kaṭaip paṭṭavaṉākka muyaṉṟatu, avar ceyta tīviṉaiyāl avaratu ciṟṟaṟivutāṉum āṇavavalliruḷiṟ putaintu mayaṅkiṉa maiyiṉaiyē viḷaṅkak kāṭṭukiṉṟatu; vaṭamoḻiyaṟiyāta potu makkaḷai ēmāṟṟu taṟporuṭṭu vaiṇavar mēlai iraṇṭām mantirattiṟkuk kaṭṭiccoṉṉa poruḷ vaṭamoḻiyuṇarntār etiril veṟum pāḻāy muṭiyumeṉka.
nāyakaravarkaḷ ivviṭattē curuṅkac cila coṟkaḷil maṟuttukkūṟiya meyyurai meypporuḷ potintatātalai mēlē yāmuñ curukkamākavē viḷakkik kāṭṭiṉām. itaṉvirivai yām iyaṟṟiya māṇikkavācakar varalāṟuṅ kālamum, caivacittānta ñāṉapōtam, tamiḻarmatam eṉṉum virintanūlkaḷiṟ kaṇṭu koḷka.
iṉic, civaperumāṉē vēḷvimutalvaṉ (yākapati) āvaṉ eṉṉum uṇmaiyiṉai āriyattil mikap paḻaiyanūlākiya irukkuvētatti lirunteṭutta kātapatim mētapatim rutram jalāṣapēṣajam, tatcamyō: camnam īmahē (1, 43, 4) eṉṉum mantirattāl nāyakaravarkaḷ naṉku valiyuṟuttik kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. im mantiramāṉatu, `pāṭṭukaḷukkut talaivaṉum vēḷvikaḷukku mutalvaṉum, nōytīrkkum maruntukaḷukku uṭaiyavaṉum āṉa uruttiraṉatu tiruvaruḷaip peṟumporuṭṭu avaṉai nāṭukiṉṟōm eṉap poruḷpayantu civaperumāṉē pāṭṭukaḷukkum vēḷvikaḷukkun talaivaṉātaluṭaṉ, avaṉē mummalap piṇitīrkkum maruntukaḷ uṭaiyāṉumāy (vaittiya nātaṉāy)t tikaḻtalaiyum naṉku viḷakkik kāṭṭutal niṉaiviṟ patikkaṟpāṟṟu. ivvirukkuvēta mantirattiṉuñ ciṟantatum paḻamaiyāvatum piṟitillai. iṉṉum av virukkuvētamē, āvōrājā namatvarayarutram...... avasēkruṇutvam (4, 3, 1) eṉṉum mantirattāl vēḷvikaḷukku vēntaṉāvāṉ uruttiraṉēyeṉṟum, uṅkaḷaik kākkumporuṭṭu avaṉaiyē nāṭutal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟatu. iṉṉum, atu civaperumāṉai viḷittu irupattoru vēḷvikaḷum ummiṭattē vaikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa (1, 72, 6) eṉap pakarkiṉṟatu. mēlum, akniyērutraṉ eṉa av vētam pukalvatālum, ataṟkiṇaṅkavē tirunāvukkaracunāyaṉārum.
eriperukkuvar avveri īcaṉa
  turuvaruk kamatāva tuṇarkilār
eṉa aruḷicceytiruttalāṉum, tīyaiyiṉṟi vēḷvi vēṭṭal iṉmaiyāṉum, vēḷviyeṉpatu oḷivaṭivāka viḷaṅkum uruttiraṉait tīvaṭiviṉkaṇ vaittu vaḻipāṭu āṟṟutalē yaṉṟi vēṟiṉmaiyāṉum yākapati civaperumāṉēyāvaṉeṉa irukkuvētam palakālum eṭuttu vaṟpuṟutturaittatu vāyvatē yām eṉka. ivvāṟṟāl, vēḷvimutalvaṉān taṉmai civaperumāṉukkē urittāvataṉṟi, nampōṟ piṟantiṟakkum māyavaṉ nāṉmukaṉ mutalāṉa ēṉaiyōrkku urittākāmai yuṇarntu kaṭaippiṭikka.
iṉṉam, evarevar ententat tēvarkaḷai nōkki vēḷvi vēṭpiṉum, avai tammaiyellām vēḷvi mutalvaṉāṉa civapirāṉē ēṟṟu, aṅṅaṉam ēṟkumāṟṟāl at tēvarkaḷiṉ uyirkkuyirāy niṟkun tāṉē avarkaḷai uvappippaṉ eṉṉum uṇmaiyiṉaiyum piṉṉar nāyaka ravarkaḷ naṉku viḷakkiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. aruccuṉaṉ tāṉ civapirāṉ vaḻipāṭu ceytaṟkuriya kālam aṇmutaluñ, civaliṅka vaṭivam illāk kuṟaiyiṉai niṉaintu varuntak, kaṇṇaṉ taṉṉaiyē civaliṅkavaṭivāyk karuti vaḻipāṭu āṟṟuka eṉṟu kaṟpikka, avaṉum aṅṅaṉamē ceytu, piṉṉart tirukkailāyañ ceṉṟu civapirāṉ tiruvuruvattai nōkkiyavaḻit, tāṉ kaṇṇaṉmēliṭṭa pōtukaḷ attaṉaiyuñ civapirāṉ tirumēṉimēl iruttal kaṇṭu viyappuṟṟaṉaṉ eṉa māpārataṅ kūṟutaliṉ, entat tēvarai nōkki evvaḻipāṭu ceyyiṉum, av vaḻipāṭṭai at tēvariṉ uyirkkuyirāy niṟkum mātēvaṉē ēṟṟu aruḷceyvaṉeṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
iṉṉum, uruttiramūrttiyākiya civaperumāṉai, intiraṉ nāṉmukaṉ nārāyaṇaṉ mutalāṉa ēṉaic ciṟuteyvaṅkaḷōṭu oppavaittu vaṇaṅkutalālun tīya vāḻttutalālum aḻaittalālum avaratu cīṟṟattiṟku yām āḷākamāṭṭōm eṉak kiḷakkum irukkuvēta iraṇṭām maṇṭilattu 33-ām patikattu nāṉkām mantiramākiya,
mātvā rutra cukrutāma namōpir mātuṣṭutī
  vriṣapamā sahūti
eṉpataṉai eṭuttukkāṭṭiya nāyakaravarkaṭkuc caivavulakam yātu kaimmā ṟiyaṟṟavallatu? ivviraṇṭām maṇṭilattu mūṉṟām mantiram,
cirēṣṭō jātayarutra criyā sivatama
  tavasām vajra pāhō
eṉpatu uruttiramūrttiyē ellāt tēvarkaḷiṉum mēlāṉa pukaḻuṭaiyareṉavum, valiyavarkaḷ ellāriṉum mēlāṉa valimai vāyntavareṉavum naṉkeṭuttuk kūṟutalāl, uruttiracivattiṟku mēlāṉa allatu īṭāṉa teyvam ētumē illaiyeṉa irukkuvētamē aṟutiyiṭṭu viḷakkutalālum, iv vētattiṉuñ ciṟanta paḻaiya vaṭanūl ētumillāmai yālum, paṇṭaik kālantoṭṭu nammintumakkaḷ civaperumāṉai oḷivaṭivil vaittu vaṇaṅkivarutalālum avaṉait tavira vēṟoru teyvattai vēḷvimutalvaṉ (yākapati) eṉak kōṭal avarkkuṭam pāṭākāmai uḷḷaṅkai nellikkaṉipōṟ ṟeḷḷitil viḷaṅkā niṟkumeṉka.
iṅṅaṉamāka ellāt tēvarkaḷiṉum uyarnta civaperumāṉē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷum vēḷvikaḷukkut talaivaṉum ātalāl, entat tēvarai nōkki ettakaiya vēḷvi vēṭpiṉum mutaliṟ civapirāṉai aḻaittu avaṟku aviyuṇavu celuttiya piṉṉarē, maṟṟait tēvarai varuvittu avarkku aviyuṇavu tarum vaḻakkam vēḷviyāciriyarāṟ kaiyāḷappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. itaṉālum, uruttiraṉē vēḷvimutalvaṉeṉpatu peṟappaṭāniṟkum.
iṉṉum, nāṉmukaṉ māyaṉ intiraṉ mutalāṉa tēvarkaḷiṉ tuṇaiyai nampic civaperumāṉmēṟ pakaikoṇṭu tarukkiya takkaṉ, civaperumāṉai ikaḻntu otukkic ceyta peruvēḷviyāṉatu, avarāl takarttu aḻikkappaṭṭa ceyti, nāl vētaṅkaḷil oṉṟāṉa kiruṣṇa yajur vētattiṉ `taittirīya caṅkitaiyilum (2, 6, 8, 3) cukla yajur vētattaic cērnta `catapata pirāmaṇattilum (1, 7, 3, 1) naṉkeṭuttu nuvalappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. vaiṇava matam uṇṭātaṟkup pallāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa ip paḻaiya nūlkaḷiṟ collappaṭṭa ic ceyti, civapirāṉē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷātalai naṉku niṟuvi, avaṉai vilakkiyō allatu avaṉai maṟantō maṟṟait tēvarai nōkkic ceyyum ettakaiya vēḷviyum aḻintu pāḻātalait teṟṟeṉa viḷakkā niṟkiṉṟatu. iv vētanūṟ poruḷukku muḻutum ottē, iṭaikkālat teḻunta māpārata anucācaṉa parvamum an nikaḻcciyiṉaik kūṟukiṉṟatu; atu varumāṟu:
kaṇṇapirāṉ, tarumaṉ putalvaṉāṉa utiṭṭiraṉai nōkkik kūṟuvāṉ: caṭaimuṭiyait tāṅkiṉavarāṉa makātēvaraik kīḻvīḻntu vaṇaṅki, yāṉpeṟṟa pēṟṟiṉaiyum perumpukaḻaiyum numakkeṭuttup pukalkiṉṟēṉ. ō vēntaṉē, kēḷum, yāṉ kālaiyil eḻuntavuṭaṉē kaikaḷaic cērttuk kūppikkoṇṭu catarutrīyattai maṉa vorumaiyuṭaṉ pārāyaṇañ ceykiṉṟēṉ. ciṟanta aṭiyavaṉāṉa pirācāpati tāṉ āṟṟiya tavamuṭivil avvaṇakkavuraiyiṉai uṇṭāk kiṉaṉ. caṅkaraṉē iyaṅku muyir, niṟkumuyir ellāvaṟṟaiyun tōṟṟuvittaṉaṉ. ō maṉṉaṉē, makātēvaṉukku mēlāṉatu ētumē yillai; ēṉeṉṟāl, im mūvulakaṅkaḷilum avaṉē ellāriṉum mēlāṉavaṉ. im mēlāṉa tēvaṉukkumuṉ etirttu niṟkavallatum ētumē yillai: ēṉeṉṟāl, im mūvulakiṉum, avaṉukku oppāṉavarum evarumēyillai. pōr muṉaiyil, avaṉukkuc ciṟitu ciṉattai mūṭṭiṉālum, avaṉaip pakaittavar naṭukkam eyti uṇarviḻantu kīḻē viḻuntu viṭukiṉṟaṉar; perumpālum uyir pōkkavum paṭukiṉṟaṉar. naṭuṅkattakka avaṉatu kural muḻakkam iṭi yēṟṟiṉ muḻakkattai ottatāyiruttaliṉ, ataṉaik kēṭṭa tēvarkaḷiṉ neñcamum aḻintu paṭukiṉṟatu. tēvarkaḷā yiṉum, acurarkaḷāyiṉum, kantaruvarkaḷāyiṉum, paṉṉakar kaḷāyiṉum, vēṟevarāyiṉum avaraip, pināka pāṇiyāṉa makātēvar tamatu ceyirtta vuruvattoṭu nōkkukiṉṟārō, avarkaḷ tammai eṅkē oḷittuk koṇṭālum, avar cīṟṟaṅ koḷḷumpōtu avarkaḷ maṉaamaitiyuṭaṉ iruttal iyalātu. takkap piracāpati yāṉavaṉ vēḷvi vēṭkap pukuntu, ataṟkāṅ kiriyaikaḷai oḻuṅkupaṭuttiya kālaiyil, makātēvar cīṟṟaṅkoṇṭu avaṉatu vēḷvi yiṉai aḻikkak karutit, tamatu villiṉiṉṟum oru kaṇaiyiṉai viṭuttu, ōr uratta ōcaiyiṉai viḷaittaṉar. tēvarkaḷ ataṉaik kēṭṭu uḷaṅ kalaṅkic cōrvuṟṟaṉar. uṭaṉē, vēḷviyāṉatu aḻintamaiyālum, makātēvar perituñ cīṟṟaṅ koṇṭamaiyālum, avaratu villiṉ nāṇ ōcaikēṭṭu ellā vulakaṅkaḷum nilaitaṭumāṟiṉa; tēvarkaḷum acurarkaḷum tuṇaiyaṟṟavarkaḷāyk kīḻē vīḻntaṉar; nīrnilaikaḷ kuḻampiṉa; nilam atirntatu; malaikaḷ nuṟuṅkiṉa; vāṉam palamukamāyp piḷavuṇṭatu; iruḷāl mūṭappaṭṭu ulakaṅkaḷ oḷi ilavāyiṉa; oḷitarum ulakaṅkaḷum ñāyiṟum oḷi avintaṉa. itaṉāl, mikavum uḷam maruṇṭa muṉivarkaḷ tamatu naṉmaiyaiyum ēṉaiyellā uyirkaḷiṉ naṉmaiyaiyuṅ kōri, makātēvariṉ cīṟṟattait taṇippāṉ vēṇṭi maṟaimoḻikaḷaic colli vaḻuttiṉar. appōtu, añcattakka āṟṟaluṭaiyavarāṉa uruttirar, tēvarkaḷpāl ōṭip, peruñ cīṟṟamuṭaiyarāyp pakaṉuṭaiya kaṇkaḷai veḷiyē teṟikkumpaṭi puṭaittaṉar. mēluñ ciṉam mūṇṭu tamatu tiruvaṭiyāṟ, pūṣaṉ vēḷvip paliyait tiṉṟukoṇṭirukkaiyil, avaṉai eṭṭi yutaittu avaṉuṭaiya paṟkaḷait takarttaṉar. tēvarkaḷ appōtu naṭukkamuṟṟuc caṅkaraṉai nilattē kiṭantu paṇintaṉar. ataṉālum avaṉ ciṉam aṭaṅkāṉāyt, taṉatu vilnāṇil naṉku tīṭṭi miḷirum oru kaṇaiyiṉait toṭuttaṉaṉ. uruttirap perumāṉiṉ varampilāṟṟalaik kaṇṭa tēvarkaḷum muṉivarkaḷum peruntikilkoṇṭu, avaṉatu cīṟṟattait taṇippāṉ vēṇṭik kūppiya kaiyiṉarāyc `catarutrīyattaic colli avaṉait toḻutaṉar. uruttiraṉum tēvarkaḷatu vaḻutturaiyiṉaic cevimaṭuttu uḷam uvantaṉaṉ. at tēvarkaḷun tām vēṭṭa vēḷvip paliyiṟ ciṟanta oru paṅkiṉai avaṟku aḷittu, accattuṭaṉ avaṉaiyē aṭaikkalamāka aṭaintaṉar. ataṉpiṟ perumāṉ uḷam uvantu av vēḷviyiṉaic cīrtirutti, ataṉkaṇ aḻivuṇṭavarkaḷaiyellām mīḷa uyirppittu muṉpōl irukka aruḷ ceytaṉaṉ.
ivvāṟu, tarumaputtiraṉāṉa utiṭṭiraṉukkuk kiruṣṇa pakavāṉ eṭuttuk kūṟiya meyyuraiyāṟ civaperumāṉē vēḷvi mutalvaṉātalum, avaṉai vaḻuttātu ceyyum ettakaiya vēḷviyum payaṉṟarātu aḻitaluṭaṉ, av vēḷviyiṉai āṟṟuvōrum aḻintupaṭutalun teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkutal kāṇka.
aṟṟāyiṉum, māpāratattiṟ pōnta kaṇṇaṉatu aṟivuraiyil viṣṇuvum piramāvum nuvalappaṭāmaiyiṉ, avviruvarum uruttiraṉilum mēlāṉa kaṭavuḷareṉavum, avviruvarum uruttiraṉāl oṟukkappaṭutaṟku eḷiyaralla reṉavuṅ koḷḷāmō veṉiṟ; koḷḷām; eṉṉai? paḻaiya vētanūlkaḷaiyum pirāmaṇaṅkaḷaiyum āḻn tārāyntu pārātavarkaḷē aṅṅaṉam uraipparaṉṟi, avaṟṟait tīra ārāyntu pārttu, viṣṇuvum piramaṉum ap paḻaiya vētanūlkaḷiṟ ciṟanta teyvaṅkaḷāka eṭuttuc collappaṭāmaiyiṉālēyē, an nūlkaḷai yoṭṭit takkaṉ vēḷviyiṉaik kūṟiya māpāratamum avviruvaraiyum aṅku oru poruṭṭāka varuvittu vaittu uraiyātāyiṟ ṟeṉṟu ataṉ uṇmai kaṇṭavar aṅṅaṉam uraiyārāka liṉeṉka. irukku mutalāṉa paḻaiya vētanūlkaḷiṟ, piṉṟaik kālap purāṇa viṣṇuvaip paṟṟiya kuṟippuc ciṟitumē kāṇappaṭavillai. irukkuvētattil viṣṇuveṉṉum peyarāl aḻaikkappaṭṭavaṉ pakalavaṉē yallāmal māyavaṉ allaṉ. pakalavaṉ kālai naṇpakal mālai eṉṉum mūṉṟu poḻutukaḷilum iyaṅkum iyakkattiṉaiyē viṣṇu mūṉṟaṭiyāl ulakiṉai aḷappatāka irukkuvētam nuvalukiṉṟateṉa annūlukku urai vakutta paḻaiya urai yāciriyarāṉa avurṇavāpar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. cākapūṇi eṉṉum uraiyāciriyar, viṣṇuveṉṉuñ col nilavulakattē kāṇappaṭun tī vaṭiviṉaiyum, vāṉiṉkaṭ ṭōṉṟum pakalavaṉaiyum, iṭaiveḷiyiṟ kāṇum miṉvaṭiviṉaiyum uṇarttā niṟkiṉṟateṉa uraikkiṉṟaṉar. vētanūlkaḷukku `niruktam eḻutiya yākācāriyārum ap paḻaiya uraikārar iruvar karuttiṉaiyun tamatu nūluḷ (12, 19) naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭi, viṣṇuveṉṉuñ col oḷivaṭiviṉaiyaṉṟip piṟitetaṉaiyum uṇarttāmai naṉku teḷivittār. iṉi, vētam, pirāmaṇam, upaniṭatam, itikācam, purāṇam mutalāṉa vaṭamoḻinūlkaḷai eḻutteṇṇi yārāynta āṅkilap pērāciriyarāṉa miyūr (Dr. J. Muir) eṉṉun turaimakaṉār tām an nūlkaḷai āḻntārāynta ārāycciyiṉ muṭipāka viṣṇuvaik kuṟitteḻutiya karutturaiyiṉai īṇṭu moḻipeyarttu varaikiṉṟām:
muṟceṉṟa pakkaṅkaḷil eṭuttuk kāṭṭap paṭṭirukkum mēṟkōḷuraikaḷāl, irukku vētattilēṉum pirāmaṇaṅ kaḷilēṉum viṣṇu (māyavaṉ) oru muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāka eṇṇappaṭavillaiyeṉpatu teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkā niṟkiṉṟatu. ip paḻaiya nūlkaḷil avar tēvarkaḷil oruvarāka maṭṭuṅ karutappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉarē yallāmal maṟṟaiyōrukku mēlāṉavarākak karutappaṭavē yillai. niruktattiliruntu (12, 19) yāṉ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyirukkum mēṟkōḷuraikaḷāl; yākācāri yārāvatu, avarāl eṭuttuk kāṭṭap paṭṭavarum vētaṅkaḷukku uraikaḷ vakutta paḻaiya āciriyarumāṉa cākapūṇi, avurṇavāpar eṉpavarkaḷāvatu intiya tēvar kuḻāttavarkaḷaiviṭa uyarnta oru nilaiyiṉai viṣṇuvukkuk koṭukkavillai yeṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākā niṟkum. (Dr. J. Muir’s Original Sanscrit Textsï Vol. IV, (1873) p. 156.
iṅṅaṉam vētanūlkaḷai muṟṟum ārāyntu naṭunilai vāḻātu muṭittuk kūṟiya āṅkilap pērāciriyariṉ meyyuraiyāl vēta kālattil viṣṇu ōr uyarnta tēvarākak karutappaṭāmai uṟutiyākap peṟappaṭukiṉṟa taṉṟō? mēlum, vētakālattai yaṭuttup pirāmaṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiya kālattilum viṣṇu ōr uyarnta kaṭavuḷākak koḷḷappaṭāmai kāṭṭutum: viṣṇu tēvarkaḷiṉ utaviyāl tāṉ peṟṟa veṟṟiyiṉaip potuvākak karutāmaṟ ṟaṉakkē yuriyatākak karuti iṟumāntu nāṇēṟṟiya taṉatu villiṉ mēṉmuṉaiyiṉmēl taṉatu mōvāyiṉai aḻuttikkoṇṭu niṟpa, atukaṇṭa maṟṟait tēvarkaḷ cerukkuṟṟa avaṉaik kolvāṉ vēṇṭi eṟumpukaḷai ēva, avai av villiṉ aṭiyiṟ pūṭṭappaṭṭa nāṇaik kaṭittu aṟukkavē, av villiṉ mēṉmuṉai mikka vicaiyuṭaṉ nimirntu, avaṉatu talai aṟupaṭṭut tolaivē ceṉṟu viḻac ceytatu. eṉṉum ic ceyti `catapata pirāmaṇattilum (14, 1, 1, 1) `taittirīya āraṇyakattilum (5, 1, 1-7), `pañcavimca pirāmaṇattilum (7, 5, 6) naṉkeṭuttu nuvalappaṭukiṉṟatu. viṣṇu ōr uyarnta tēvaṉākavāvatu, muḻumutaṟkaṭavuḷāka vāvatu iruntāl aṅṅaṉam avaṉ talaiyaṟupaṭṭu vīḻtal ākāmaiyāṟ, paḻaiya kālattil viṣṇu oru ciṟanta tēvaṉākat tāṉuṅ koḷḷappaṭavillai yeṉṉum uṇmai uḷḷaṅ kai nellikkaṉipōl teḷḷitiṉ viḷaṅkā niṟkumeṉka.
iṉi, nāṉmukaṉākiya piramāvum ōr uyarnta kaṭavuḷ alla ṉeṉpatūum vētanūlkaḷāl naṉkaṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. piramaṉ tāṉ paṭaitta taṉmakaḷ caracuvati mika aḻakiyaḷāy iruttal kaṇṭu avaḷmēṟ peruṅkāmaṅ koṇṭu avaḷaip puṇarntaṉaṉ. ataṉaik kaṇṭu avaṉmēṟ ciṉaṅkoṇṭa tēvarkaḷ, taṉmakaḷaip puṇarntu takāta ceyta nāṉmukaṉai oṟukkumpaṭi uruttirapperumāṉai vēṇṭa, avar avaṉaip pācupatak kaṇaiyāṟ kutti oṟuttaṉar. eṉṉum ic ceyti `catapata pirāmaṇattilum (1, 7, 4, 1), `aitarēya pirāmaṇattilum, `maṟca purāṇattiluṅ kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. itaṉāl, nāṉmukaṉ muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākāmaiyum, avaṉ civa pirāṉāl oṟukkap paṭṭamaiyum, avaṉ tēvarkaḷil oruvaṉē yātalum mikap paḻaiya vēta nūlkaḷilēyē peṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
iṅṅaṉamāka, viṣṇuvum piramaṉun tēvarkaḷuṭ ciṟanta varākap paḻaiya vētakālattuc cāṉṟōrkaḷāṟ koḷḷap paṭāmaiyiṉ, takkaṉ vēṭṭa vēḷvik kaḷattil avarkaḷiru varaiyumpaṟṟiya ceyti ētum paḻaiya vētanūlkaḷiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭila teṉṟaṟintukoḷka. paḻaiya vētanūl kaḷilellām uruttira civattiṉ muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuṭ ṭaṉmaiyē naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭiruttal mēṟpōnta vētanūṉ mēṟkōḷkaḷāl iṉitu viḷaṅkāniṟkumeṉpatu.
iṉi, vētanūlkaḷukkup pallāyira āṇṭu piṟpaṭṭuc caiva vaiṇava mataṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyakālaiyil, am mataṅkaḷai mēṟkoṇṭa kurukkaṇmār tattaṅ kaṭavuḷarai uyarttal vēṇṭip paṭaitteḻutiya  caiva vaiṇava purāṇaṅkaḷil maṭṭumē takkaṉvēṭṭa vēḷviyil viṣṇuvum piramaṉum vantirunta ceyti putitupuṉaintu nuḻaikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. atuvēyumaṉṟi, vētanūlkaḷuḷ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum mika muntiyatum mikap paḻaiyatumāṉa irukkuvētam (1, 43, 1),
kātapatim mētapatim rutram jalāṣapēṣajam
  tatsamyō: camnam īmahē
  ya: sukra: ivacūryō hiraṇyam ivarōcatē
  rēṣṭō tēvānām vacu:
eṉṉum mantirattāl, pāṭṭukaḷukkut talaivarum, vēḷvi kaḷukkut talaivarum, nōytīrkkum maruntukaḷai vaittiruppa varum āṉa uruttiramūrttiyai, avartamatu iṉṉaruḷaip peṟumporuṭṭu nāṭukiṟōm: pakalavaṉaippōṟ pēroḷi yuṭaṉ viḷaṅkupavarum, poṉṉaippōl tikaḻpavarum, ellāt tēvarkaḷiṉuñ ciṟantavarum varam aḷippavarumāṉa avarpāl itaṉaip peṟavēṇṭukiṟōm eṉṟu civapirāṉai muḻumutaṟ peruṅkaṭavuḷākavaittu vaḻuttiyirukka, mataveṟipiṭitta piṟkālattavarkaḷ, kaṭavuḷar allāta viṣṇu piramaṉ eṉṉum iruvarōṭu uruttirap perumāṉaiyum oṉṟucērttu, am mūvaraiyum mummūrttikaḷākkip purāṇa kataikaḷ palavaṟṟaip paṭaittu matac caṇṭaikaḷaip palka vaittut tīviṉaikkāḷā yiṉar. itukāṟum viḷakkiyavāṟṟāl muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākiya civapirāṉē vēḷvikaḷukkut talaivarāyiruntu aruḷ ceypava rallāmal mutaṟkaṭavuḷ allāta māyavaṉum nāṉmukaṉum yaññapatikaḷāyiruntu aruḷceyyamāṭṭuvār allareṉa nāyakaravarkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭiya uṇmai aṟiñarkaḷ uḷḷattiṟ patikkaṟpālatām perumāṭci yuṭaiyatātal kaṇṭukoḷka.

14. civaniṉmāliyam
iṉic, civapirāṉukkup paṭaitta paṇṭaṅkaḷait tūyarāyiruppavarkaḷ nukartalākāteṉṟum, avaṟṟait tūyar allātavarkaḷē nukartaṟkuriyareṉṟum vacaikūṟiya vaiṇavarurai poruntāp poyyuraiyātalai nāyakaravar kaḷ kāṭṭuvāṉ pukuntu, civapirāṉukkup paṭaitta uṇavaiyē aruntal vēṇṭum, uruttiraṉukku ēṟpitta nīraiyum pālaiyumē parukal vēṇṭum, civaperumāṉukkuc cārttiya maṇapporuḷ malarkaḷaiyē aṇintukoḷḷal vēṇṭum eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭa jāpāla upaniṭatattaiyum, ataṉait taḻīik kūṟiya `iliṅka purāṇa `piramāṇṭapurāṇa uraikaḷaiyum mēṟkōḷkaḷāka eṭuttu viḷakki yirukkiṉṟaṉar.
mēlum, uruttirapperumāṉukkup paṭaittavaikaḷait tūyarāyiruppavarkaḷē nukaraṟpālaraṉṟit, tūyar allāta varkaḷ nukaraṟpālarallareṉak kāmikākamamum `caiva purāṇamuṅ kūṟum uraikaḷaiyum nāyakaravarkaḷ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar. ivvāṟṟāṟ, civapirāṉukkup paṭaitteṭutta `niṉmāliyam tūyarkku ākā teṉṟun, tūyarallātārkkē āvateṉṟuṅ kūṟiya vaiṇavarurai puraipaṭṭup poyyā yoḻital kāṇka. caivacamayattaip puṟampaḻittaṟ poruṭṭu māyāvātikaḷum vaiṇavarkaḷum piṟkālattē puṉain teḻutiya poynūṟ poruḷkoṇṭu, paḻaiya vētānta nūlākiya jāpāla upaniṭatam uyarttup pēciya civaniṉmā liyattai iḻittup pēcuvār tīviṉaiyāḷarāvarēyaṉṟi, nalviṉaiyāḷar āvarallareṉpatu kaṭaippiṭikka. iṉṉum itupaṟṟi nāyakaravarkaḷ viritteḻutiyirukkum pakutikaḷai īṇṭeṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉ itu mika viriyumātaliṉ itaṉai ivvaḷavil niṟuttukiṉṟām.

15. kaṇṇaṉ piṟavi
iṉit, tirumālē kaṇṇaṉākap piṟaviyeṭuttār eṉa vaiṇavar cilar viṭāppiṭiyāyk kūṟik, kaṇṇaṉai vaḻipaṭṭavar vīṭupēṟṟai eytiṉareṉṟum, pittaṉāṉa civaṉai vaḻipaṭṭavar vīṭupēṟṟai eytāmal uruttira patattaiyē peṟṟaṉareṉṟuṅ karaintataṉai nāyakaravarkaḷ maṟuttirukkum pakutiyum niṉaiviṟ patikkaṟpāla toṉṟā yirukkiṉṟatu.
tirumālē kaṇṇaṉākap piṟantāreṉṟu paḻaiya nūlākiya māpāratam uraippak kāṇēm. maṟṟu, annūliṉ `ātīparvat tiṉkaṇṇē (7306) tēvarkaḷ tamakku acurar kaḷāl nērum iṭukkaṇ tīrppāṉvēṇṭi viṣṇuvaik kuṟaiyirappa, avar tamatuṭampiṉkaṇ uḷḷa kaṟuppumayir oṉṟaiyum veḷḷaimayiroṉṟaiyum piṭuṅkip pōkka, avai yatukulattavarākiya tēvaki, urōkiṇi eṉṉum mātar iruvar karuppaiyiṉuḷḷum nuḻaintu veḷḷaimayir palatēvaṉākavuṅ, kaṟuppumayir kiruṣṇaṉākavum piṟantaṉa veṉṉum varalāṟu nuvalappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu kaṇṇaṉ palarāmaṉ piṟappukkaḷaik kūṟum māpārata uraikkiṇaṅkavē `viṣṇupurāṇamum (5, 1, 12) avartam avvaralāṟṟiṉaik kūṟāniṟkiṉṟatu. itaṉāl, viṣṇuvē kaṇṇaṉum palarāmaṉumākap piṟantilar; avartam mayirkaḷ iraṇṭē avviruvarumākap piṟantaṉaveṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅki niṟkiṉṟatu. ākavē, viṣṇuvē kaṇṇaṉākap piṟantaṉa ṉeṉṉum vaiṇavariṉ kūṟṟu, paḻaiya nūl varalāṟṟukku muṟṟum muraṇāyp poypaṭṭoḻital kāṇka. mēlum, innūl varalāṟṟiṉāl viṣṇu viṉuṭampil naraimayirum uḷateṉpatu peṟappaṭutalāl, avar narai tirai mūppup piṇi cākkāṭṭukku uḷḷākum. makkaḷ yākkaiyuṭaiyavarē yallāmal, ak kuṟṟaṅkaḷillā oḷiyuṭampu vāynta muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākareṉpatu tāṉē pōtarum; maṟṟuc civaperumāṉō naraitirai  mutalāṉa kuṟṟaṅkaḷ ciṟitum aṇukappeṟā oḷiyuruviṉareṉṟu mikappaḻaiya irukkuvētam mutaṟ ṟoṭarpāka vanta ellā nūlkaḷum oru mukamāy niṉṟu otturaippak kāṇṭumaṉṟi, avar akkuṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaiyarāyp piṟantārla intāreṉṟu yāṇṭum uraippakkāṇkilēm. aṅṅaṉamāka makkaḷ yākkaiyiṉarāṉa viṣṇuvaiyum avaratu kaṟuppu mayiriṉ piṟaviyāṉa kaṇṇaṉaiyum nikaḻum muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāṉa civapirāṉ toḻutār uruttiravattiṉai yetuvareṉayum puraṭṭippēcip, poyyai meyyākavum, meyyaip poyyākavum tirittuviṭa muyaṉṟa vaiṇavariṉ muyaṟci uṇmai yārāyccikku muṉ pañcāyp paṟantoḻital kāṇka.
cōmacuntara nāyakar varalāṟu 
- muṟṟum -

PREFACE
The religious and philosophical matter that appears in the following pages form the substance of my presidential speech delivered on the 15th October, 1927, at the 42nd annual meetingof the Saiva Siddhanta Sabha at Trichi Rock Fort. What was briefly treated in the course of the delivery is now expanded into its full final form; and to a few Puranic legends then quoted to illustrate the degenerated notions about God and religion, some more are now added to make the treatment of the subject complete within its bounds. The object of this little book is to disclose the vast difference that lies between the ancient form of Saiva religion and its modern form.
In very ancient times the theoretical side of Saivism had been purely philosophical. while its practical side/ which consisted in bringing into an intimate relation of love the human and the Divine selves, tended to a worship of luminous bodies such as the Sun and the Moon and also fire which constitutes the essence of all bright objects. God was conceived to be a subtle spiritual light which manifests itself in the chief physical luminary the Sun, so that all rites, religious observances, and original myths in Saivism clung around this resplendent body. For us who have come to think of the appearances of the sun and the moon as so many natural occurrences that uniformly take place in the realm of dead matter, they have lost their high purpose and significance. But to  our ancestors who lived in close relationship with Nature, they had been a great wonder, and to their then more clear, vivid, quick  and penetrating intuitive sense they revealed the gracious presence of a loving heavenly Father. What they preceived by means of their intuiton, and what they loved so ardently in the manifestation of light, had not been a mere figment of their imagination, but a reality informed with infinite love and intelligence and capable of readily responding to the prayers of a fervent heart. It is this love, this intelligence inthe Supreme Being which is otherwise transcendental and incomprehensible to the finite understanding of the human selves, that impels it to manifest itself to those who are pious and devoted.
Fortunately for us we are in possession of rare internalevidences in the heart-melting hymns sung by St. Manickavachakar and other Saivite Saints in glory of Lord Siva, sufficient to convince us of the reality of God’s sudden manifestation to them in tangible form. From the reference made by them in several of their hymns, we are in a position to study the essential nature of the personality of God and how it blesses the few fit, pious,  sincere souls. Except the inspired utterances of such exceptionally pure and favoured saints of Saivism, others can give us no clue to a clear, and definite conception of God’s personality. So far as my knowledge of other religious saints is concerned, I dare say that I could get not even a single glimpse from their hymns and sayings (whose genuineness though I do not call in question as modern critics and commentators do) with reference to the vision of God they had, if they had any, and the nature and description of the personality they had thus seen. But in the case of the Saiva Saints as St. Manickavachakar and St. Thirujnanas ambandha, we are extremely glad to say that we are left in no uncertainity either with regard to their authorship of their sacred hymns or with regard to the vision of God they had and the definitely clear description they give of the Hevenly Father and Mother that had come to them. When we enter the region of the sacred Saiva literature, we find ourselves to our great wonder and pleasure that we are on the safe, solid ground of genuine historical facts. From our most ancient sage Tholkappiar whose age goes back to 3500 B.C. to St, Thiruvalluvar and the founders and teachers of Saiva religion, all have left behind them their valuable literary and religious works which embody their thoughts on arts, ethics, psychology, philosophy and religion.
Although the Aryan, Buddhist and Jain immigrants came and settled in the Tamil country as early as the 1st century of the Christian era, their influence could not touch even the fringe of the Tamilian mind, So strongly did the mind of the cultured Tamil people cling to the realities of life and existence, that the Aryan rites and myths, and the Buddhist and Jain nihilism could find no entrance into it, great as had been their efforts to achieve it. But the modern Mayavada Vedanta and Vaishnavism which came to spread here after the 8th century A.D., achieved what their predecessors could not achieve, simply by fabricating fictitious, obscene, and licentious Puranic stories and thus catching the fancy of the Tamil people. This influx of the Aryan falsehood into Tamil led to the formation of a class of literature which is notorious for the most degraded type of Puranic religion Saiva, Sakta and Vaishnava. To the best of my ability I have endeavoured in the following treatise to sift this mongrel mixture and disentangle the golden truths of Saiva religion from the empty falsehoods of other novel religions. The task is no easier one and it cannot further be appreciated even by the Saivites whose mind is dyed deep in the Aryan myths. Still I have candidly executed it in the interest of the ancient, and genuine. Tamilian thought, and am emboldened in its execution by the prospect of a rising generation devoted solely to the cause of truth. May the glory of Lord Siva the almighty God of the universe shine for ever!
The Sacred Order of Love	VEDACHALAM.
Pallavaram,
12th May, 1929.

1. piṟavi eṭuttataṉ nōkkam
aṉparkaḷē!
iccapaiyār kēṭṭuk koṇṭataṟ kiṇaṅkik kaṭavuḷ nilaikku māṟāṉa koḷkaikaḷ caivam ākā eṉpataṉaip poruḷākak koṇṭu oru virivurai nikaḻttalāmeṉṟu tuṇintēṉ. ikkālanilaikku ēṟṟapaṭi, etaṉaippaṟṟip pēca lām eṉṟu ārāyntu pārkkaiyil, meyyallāta kōṭpāṭu kaḷaic caivacamayamākap piṟaḻa uṇarntu avaṟṟaiyē caiva camayameṉa nammaṉōr mayaṅkik kiṭattalāṉum, poyyai meyyāka mayaṅkik kiṭakkum varaiyil ammayakka uṇarccikku ētuvāṉa aṟiyāmai nammai viṭṭu nīṅkāmaiyiṉ nām ellāmvalla civattiṉ nilaiyai uṇarntu ip piṟavit tuṉpattai nīkkuta lākāmaiyāṉum, intap poruḷaiyē pēcuvatu iṉṟiyamaiyātatāka eṉakkut tōṉṟiyatu. ēṉeṉṟāṟ, piṟavi nīṅkātavaraiyil, appiṟaviyai nīkkum muyaṟci uṇmaiyil naṭavāta varaiyil, ammuyaṟci naṭai peṟutaṟku meyyuṇarcci peṟātavaraiyil nām ippiṟavi eṭuttataṉ nōkkam niṟaivēṟāmalē pōyviṭum.
namakku aritiṟ kiṭaittirukkum makkaṭ piṟaviyiṉ nōkkam niṟaivēṟātāyiṉ nām piṟavippayaṉai aṭuttaṭuttu iḻantu piṟavit tuṉpattiṟ kiṭantu cuḻaluvatōṭu, namakkup piṟavikaḷai ōyātu koṭut taṟku ētuvāyuḷḷa palarkkum nām mīḷāta tuṉpat taiyuṅ koṭuttavarāvōm. emmai īṉṟeṭukkun tāymār ettaṉaimuṟai emmait tam vayiṟṟakattē cumantu cumantu tuṉpuṟu kiṉṟaṉar! emmaip peṟṟeṭuttu vaḷarppataṟku ettaṉaimuṟai alakkaṇuṟu kiṉṟaṉar! itu maṭṭumō, emmaip paṭaikkum iṟaivaṉ ettaṉaimuṟai emmaip paṭaittup paṭaittuk kaicalikkiṉṟāṉ! itu paṟṟiyaṉṟō, paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷum,
mātā uṭalcalittāḷ valviṉaiyēṉ kālcalittēṉ
 vētāvuṅ kaicalittu viṭṭāṉē--nātā
 aruppaiyūr vāḻcivaṉē iṉṉumōr aṉṉaik
 karuppaiyūr vārāmaik kā
eṉṟu aruḷic ceytaṉarkaḷ. ivvulakattil nām vantu piṟantatellām meyyuṇarvu peṟutaṟkēyām. iv ulaka vāḻkkaiyāṉatu namakku oru paḷḷikkūṭāmā yirukkiṉṟatu. paḷḷikkūṭattiṟ kalvipayilum māṇavarkaḷ āciriyaṉ utavi koṇṭu meynnūlkaḷ palavaṟṟaip payiṉṟum, tammoṭu payilum māṇākkarkaḷuṭaṉ kalvittuṟaikaḷai ucāvi aiyan tīrappeṟṟum aṟivu viḷaṅki varutal pōla, nāmum iv ulaka vāḻkkait tuṟaikaḷ ovvoṉṟilum pēraṟiñar paṟpalariṉ utaviyāl uṇmaikaḷ paṟpalavaṟṟai uṇarntum, nammō ṭotta makkaḷ ōvātu ceyyum palatiṟa muyaṟcikaḷāṟ pala tiṟa vāḻkkai nalaṅkaḷaip peṟṟum nāḷukku nāḷ aṟivilum iṉpattilum mēṉmēṟ ciṟantu varukiṉṟaṉamallamō?
ivvāṟṟāl nammaip paḻamai toṭṭup paṟṟik koṇṭu varum aṟiyāmaiyum ataṉ vāyilāka varun tuṉpamum paṭippaṭiyē nīṅkap peṟṟu varukiṉṟōm. iṅṅaṉamāka aṟiyāmai nīkkamum aṟivuppēṟum aṭaitalē ippiṟavi eṭuttataṉ nōkkamāyk kāṇappaṭutaliṉ, av aṟiyāmaiyaik kaḷaintu aṟivu peṟutaṟkuriya uṇmaik kōṭpāṭukaḷaiyē nām ārāyntu kaikkoḷḷal vēṇṭum eṉpatu paṟṟik kaṭavuḷ nilaikku māṟāṉa koḷkaikaḷ caivam ākā eṉpataṉai nuṅkaḷ muṉṉilaiyiṟ pēcat tuṇintēṉ.

2. kaṭavuḷ ellāvaṟṟaiyum
kaṭantu niṟkum nilai
iṉi, eṭuttuk koṇṭa ipporuḷaik kaṭavuḷ nilai yāteṉavum, annilaikku māṟāṉa koḷkaikaḷ yāvai yeṉavum, ammāṟāṉa koḷkaikaḷ caiva mākātavāṟu yāṅṅaṉameṉavum mūṉṟuvakaiyāl ārāyntu pārttal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ārāyum iv ārāycci mika uyarnta uṇmaip poruḷkaḷait teḷivatil namatu karuttai īṭupaṭutti niṟṟalāl, ippōtu nām tava muyaṟciyaic ceyyap pōkum nilaiyil irukkiṉṟōm. ēṉeṉṟāl tavam eṉpatu uyarta poruḷil namatu karuttai orumukappaṭutti uṟaittu niṟṟalēyātalāl, ippōtu nām tava muyaṟciyait tuvaṅki niṟkiṉṟōm eṉpataṉai niṉaintu, oruvarōṭoruvar pēcāmalum maṟṟa vīṇeṇṇaṅkaḷai eṇṇāmalum nām pēcum poruḷilēyē karuttai niṟutti amaitiyāy iruttal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, mutalāvatākak kaṭavuḷ nilaiyai ārāyntu pārppōm. kaṭavuḷ eṉṉuñ collaik kēṭṭa aḷavāṉē aḵtu epporuḷaiyuṅ kaṭantu niṟpatu eṉṉum poruḷ pulaṉākā niṟkiṉṟatu. iṉik kaṭavuḷ epporuḷaik kaṭantu niṟkiṉṟār eṉṟu ārāyntu pārkkumiṭattu iv ulakat taiyum iv ulakattil vāḻum uyirkaḷaiyum kaṭantu niṟkiṉ ṟār eṉṟē nām collutal vēṇṭum. nammaṟivāl mutalil aṟiyappaṭuvatu ivulakamēyaṉṟip piṟitillai. iv ulakan tāṉ eṅṅaṉam ākkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu eṉṟu mēlum ārāyntu pārkkumiṭattu maṇṇun taṇṇīrun tīyuṅ kāṟṟum vāṉu meṉṉum aimperu mutaṟporuḷkaḷāl ākkappaṭṭi ruttal viḷaṅkum. iv uṇmaiyai iṟṟaikku aiyāyirattu ainnūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉamē irunta nam paḻan tamiḻāciri yarāṉa tolkāppiyaṉār,
nilan tī nīr vaḷi vicumpōṭu aintuṅ
  kalanta mayakkam ulakamātaliṉ.
eṉṟum, ivar kālattukku ainnūṟu āṇṭu piṟpaṭṭu vanta talaic caṅkattu nallicaip pulavarāṉa murañciyūr muṭinākarāyar, 				(puṟa - nā - 2) 
maṇtiṇinta nilaṉum
  nilaṉēntiya vicumpum
  vicumputaivaru vaḷiyum
  vaḷittalaiiya tīyun
  tīmuraṇiya nīrumeṉṟāṅku
  aimperumpūtattu iyaṟkaipōla. 
eṉṟu kūṟutal koṇṭu uṇarntu koḷḷal  vēṇṭum.
ivvāṟu aiyāyira āṟāyira āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉamē iv ulakattiṉiyalpiṉai naṉkārāyntu aintu mutaṟ poruḷ kaḷilaṭakkiya nam paḻantamiḻāciriyariṉ pēraṟivup peṟṟiyai eṉṉeṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ! eṅṅaṉam pukaḻvēṉ! aṉparkaḷē!
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam pakukkappaṭṭa ipporuḷkaḷ aintaiyum nām eṅṅaṉam aṟikiṉṟōm eṉṟāṟ, ceviyiṉ utavi koṇṭu ōcaiyai uṇarkiṉṟōm. iv ōcaiyō vāṉiṉ taṉmai. ocaiyai uṇaravē av ōcaikku nilaik kaḷaṉāyk kaṭpulaṉākāta vāṉattaiyum aṟikiṉṟōm. kaṇṇiṉutavi koṇṭu tīyiṉ taṉmaiyāṉa oḷiyai uṇarkiṉṟōm. it tīyil oḷiyēyaṉṟic cūṭu muṇṭāta liṉ ac cūṭṭiṉai uṭampi ṉellā vuṟuppukkaḷālum uṇarkiṉṟōm. iṉik kāṟṟiṉ taṉmaiyiṉaiyum aṅṅaṉamē uṭampiṉ ellā vuṟuppukkaḷālum uṇar kiṉṟōm. vāyiṉ utavi koṇṭu nīriṉ cuvaiyiṉaiyum mūkkiṉ utavikoṇṭu maṇṇiṉ nāṟṟattiṉaiyum uṇarkiṉṟōm. ivvāṟāka vāṉ vaḷi tī nīr maṇ eṉṉum aimperum poruḷkaḷaiyum nām aṟintu koḷḷutaṟku utaviyāka mey vāy kaṇ mūkkuc cevi yeṉṉum aimpoṟikaḷaiyum, av aimpoṟikaḷamainta iv vuṭampiṉaiyum ellāmvalla iṟaivaṉ namakkup paṭaittuk koṭuttirukkiṉṟār. iv aimpoṟikaḷ illāviṭṭālum allatu ivai ciṟitu paḻutupaṭṭālum nām ulakattup poruḷkaḷai aṟitalum avaṟṟāṟ payaṉ koḷḷutaluñ ciṟitum iyalā. cevi illaiyāyiṉ allatu ceviyiruntum atu paḻutupaṭṭup pōṉatāyiṉ nām ōcaiyai uṇarva teṅkē! kaṇ illaiyāyiṉ allatu kaṇṇiruntum atu paḻutupaṭṭup pōkumāyiṉ oḷiyiṉaiyum av oḷiyiṉāl viḷakkappaṭum palatiṟap poruḷvaṭivaṅ kaḷaiyum nām kāṇpa teṅkē! iṅṅaṉamē maṟṟaip poṟikaḷ illaiyāyiṉ allatu avai yiruntum paḻutupaṭṭaṉa vāyiṉ appoṟi kaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum poruḷkaḷai nām aṟintu koḷḷutal iyalātaṉṟō? aṉparkaḷē! iṅṅaṉam poṟikaḷiṉ utaviyiṉṟi aṟiya māṭṭāta nilaiyi lirukkum ēḻai makka ḷākiya nām ciṟṟaṟivuñ ciṟutoḻilumuṭaiya ciṟṟuyirkaḷē yallāmaṟ kaṭavuḷātal yāṅṅaṉam? cilar uyirkaḷē kaṭavu ḷeṉṟu vāykūcāmaṟ collukiṉṟārkaḷ. uyirkaḷō kaṭavuḷāyiṉ aimpoṟi kaḷiṉ utaviyiṉṟiyē av vuyirkaḷ aimporuḷkaḷaiyum uṇartal vēṇṭumaṉṟō? ātalāl uyirkaḷē kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum koḷkai piḻaipāṭuṭaittākum.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam aimpoṟikaḷāl aimporuḷkaḷai aṟiyumiṭattu aṅṅaṉam aṟiyum aṟivu avaṟṟai aṟinta aḷavilē aṟṟup pōkumāṉāl namakku aṟivu viḷakka muṇṭākātu; aṟiyāmai nīṅkātu; vilaṅkukaḷiṉum nām kīḻppaṭṭavarkaḷāy viṭuvōm. nām poruḷkaḷai aṟinta aṟivu avvaḷavilē aṟṟup pōkāmal nam niṉaivilē patintu niṉṟu apporuḷkaḷiṉ taṉmaikaḷai nam niṉaiyil toṭarpāka vaittu  aṟivittu varukiṉṟatu. nēṟṟu nām kēṭṭa coṟkaḷaiyum nēṟṟu nām kaṇṭa kāṭcikaḷaiyum uṇṭa uṇavukaḷaiyum nēṟṟaḷavilēyē maṟantu, iṉṟu kēṭkuñ coṟkaḷaiyuṅ kāṇuṅ kāṭcikaḷaiyum uṇṇum uṇavukaḷaiyum putitu putitāka aṟintu aṟinta an noṭiyi lēyē avai tammai maṟantu viṭuvōmāyiṉ namatu nilai evvaḷavu utaviyaṟṟatāy, evvaḷavu iraṅkattakkatāy irukkum! pārmiṉkaḷ aṉparkaḷē! iṅṅaṉam maṟavātapaṭi, ellām valla āṇṭavaṉ nām aṟintavaikaḷai maṟavāta niṉaiviṉai evvaḷavu irakkattōṭu namakku aḷittirukkiṉ ṟāṉ! nām poṟikaḷāl aṟinta poruḷkaḷ aḻintu maṟain tālum, ap poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟiya niṉaivukaḷ em uḷḷat til evvaḷavu vērūṉṟi niṟkiṉṟaṉa! yām eṅ kaṇṇetirē kāṇum im maṇṭapam oru kālattil illaiyāy maṟain tālum , itaṉaip paṟṟiya niṉaivu em akak kaṇṇetirē itaṉai emakkut teḷivuṟak kāṭṭumaṉṟō? allatu im maṇṭapattai viṭṭu vēṟōriṭattiṟ ceṉṟa piṉṉum emakku im maṇṭapattaip paṟṟiya niṉaivu varumaṉṟō? iṅṅaṉamē immakkaḷ vāḻkkaiyil ituvaraiyiṟ kaḻintupōṉa muṉṉāṭkaḷilē nām aṟintaṟintu vanta poruḷkaḷiṉ niṉaivukaḷellām nam uḷḷattil aḻuttamāyp patintu nāmoṉṟai niṉaippataṟku aṟivai orumukap paṭuttik kaṇmūṭiyirukkuṅkāl, ap paḻaiya niṉaivukaḷ oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāy oru toṭarpāyun, toṭarpiṉṟik kalaintum pala tiṟappaṭṭu māṟi māṟi varappeṟukiṉṟaṉa mallamō? itaṉālaṉṟō,
uṇpatu nāḻi uṭuppatu nāṉkumuḻam
  eṇpatu kōṭiniṉain teṇṇuvaṉa
eṉṟu auvaiyārum,
kaṇṭaṉa elām alla eṉṟukaṇ ṭaṉaiceytu
	karuvikara ṇaṅka ḷōyak
kaṇmūṭi orukaṇa mirukkaveṉ ṟāṟpāḻtta
	karmaṅkaḷ pōrāṭutē
eṉṟu tāyumāṉa aṭikaḷum aruḷicceyvārāyiṉar.
eṉṟu ittuṇai ārāyntavaḷavil nammāl aṟiyap paṭum poruḷkaḷeṉpaṉa maṇ puṉal aṉal kāl vāṉ eṉṉum ulakattup poruḷkaḷaintum, poṟikaḷiṉ vāyilāka nam uḷḷattil vantu patiyum iv aimporuḷ kaḷaip paṟṟiya niṉaivukaḷum eṉṉum iraṇṭumē yallāmal vēṟillai eṉpatu iṉitu viḷaṅkāniṟkum. ākavē, kaṭavuḷ ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyuṅ kaṭantu niṟpavareṉṟāl, nam aimpoṟikaḷukku viḷaṅkum aimperum poruḷkaḷaiyum ap poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟi yeḻum niṉaivukaḷaiyuṅ kaṭantu niṟpavareṉṟē muṭivu ceyyappaṭum. ēṉeṉṟāṟ kaṭavuḷ nāmaṟinta maṇṇil oruvarā? allatu nīril oruvarā? tīyil oruvarā? kāṟṟil oruvarā? allatu iṭaiveḷiyā yirukkiṉ ṟaṉarā? eṉṟu kēṭpōmāyiṉ evarum ip poruḷkaḷaik kaṭavuḷeṉṟu colla oruppaṭamāṭṭār. allatu ip poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟi nam uḷḷatti leḻum ettaṉaiyō kōṭi niṉaivukaḷil vanta niṉaivu kaṭavuḷākum eṉṟu kēṭṭāl, enta niṉaiviṉaiyuṅ kaṭavuḷeṉṟu colla evarum oruppaṭamāṭṭār. eṉavē, ulakattup poruḷkaḷ etaṉuḷ ḷuṅ kaṭavuḷ aṭaṅkāmal, avaikaḷaiyellām avar kaṭantu niṟpavar eṉpatē ellā makkaṭkum uṭampāṭāṉa potu uṇmaiyākum. nam camayāciriyarāṉa tirunāvukkaracu nāyaṉārum,
virikatir ñāyiṟallar matiyallar vēta
	vitiyallar viṇṇu nilaṉun
  tiritaru vāyuvallar ceṟitīyu mallar
	teḷinīru mallar 
eṉṟu ivvuṇmaiyai iṉitu viḷakki aruḷic ceyti rukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
iṉi, ivaikaḷaiyellām iṟaivaṉ kaṭantu niṟpavaṉ eṉṟāl, enta vakaiyiṉāl ivaikaḷaik kaṭantu niṟpavaṉ eṉpatu ārāyntu teḷital vēṇṭum. kaṭattaleṉpatai mūṉṟu vakaiyākap pākupaṭuttal vēṇṭum. avai: iṭattaik kaṭappatuṅ, kālattaik kaṭappatum, poruṭ ṭaṉmaikaḷaik kaṭappatum eṉpaṉavām. avaṟṟuḷ iṭattaik kaṭappatāvatu poruḷkaḷellāmirukkum ellā iṭattaiyuṅ kaṭantu niṟpatēyākum. nammā laṟiyappaṭṭa entap poruḷkaḷiṉ ellaiyil allatu enta iṭattiṉ ellaiyiṟ kaṭavuḷai aṭakkalām eṉṟu evaraik kēṭpiṉuṅ kaṭavuḷ entap poruḷiṉ ellaiyilum aṭaṅkār, enta iṭattiṉ ellaiyilum aṭaṅkār eṉṟē viṭai kūṟā niṟpar. ātalāṟ kaṭavuḷ iṭattaik kaṭantu niṟpavar eṉpatu ellā makkaṭkum oppa muṭintatēyākum.
iṉik, kālattaik kaṭantavar eṉpatu mukkālattaiyuṅ kaṭantu niṟpavar eṉpatēyām. avar entak kālattil tōṉṟiṉavar, entakkālattil vaḷarkiṉṟavar, entak kālattil muṭivaṭaipavar eṉṟu evaraik kēṭpiṉum, avar orukālattiṟ ṟōṉṟiyavarumallar, maṟṟorukālattil vaḷarpavarumallar, piṟitorukālattil muṭipavarumallar eṉṟē ellārum viṭai kūṟāniṟpar. atu paṟṟiyē,
ellār piṟappum iṟappumiyaṟ pāvalartam
  collāṟ ṟeḷintēmnañ cōṇēcar-illiṟ
  piṟantakatai yuṅkēḷēm pērulakil vāḻntuṇṭu
  iṟantakatai yuṅkēṭṭi lēm. 
eṉṟu āṉṟōr tirumoḻiyum eḻuntatu.
iṟaivaṉ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum muṟpaṭṭa kālantoṭṭē iruppavaṉ eṉṟu kūṟutaluṅkūṭa avaṉatu iṟaimait taṉmaikku iḻukkām eṉpatu paṟṟiyē māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ,
muṉṉaip paḻamporuṭku muṉṉaip paḻamporuḷē
  piṉṉaip putumaikkum pērttumap peṟṟiyaṉē
eṉṟum,
mūppāṉ iḷaiyavaṉ muṉṉavaṉ piṉṉavaṉ
  muppuraṅkaḷ vīppāṉ
eṉṟum aruḷic ceyvārāyiṉar.
iṉip poruḷkaḷiṉ taṉmaiyaik kaṭantavareṉpatu, avvap poruḷkaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷilun tam miyalpu aṭaṅkāmal avvellāvaṟṟiṉ iyalpukaḷaiyum muṟṟuṅ kaṭantu niṟpavar eṉpatēyām. iṉip poruḷkaḷiṉ iyalpu kaḷāvaṉa evaiyeṉṟu ārāyvōmāyiṉ, avai vaṇṇam vaṭivu aḷavu cuvai yeṉṉum nālvakaiyil aṭaṅkum. vaṇṇamāvatu civappu, nīlam, mañcaḷ, kaṟuppu, ūtā, paccai, veḷḷai eṉṉum eḻuvakai niṟaṅkaḷēyākum. vaṭivāvatu vaṭṭam, nāṟkōṇam, mukkōṇam mutaliyaṉavākum. aḷavāvatu eṇṇalaḷavai, eṭuttalaḷavai, mukattalaḷavai, nīṭṭalaḷavaiyeṉa nāṉkām. cuvaiyāṉatu tittippu, puḷippu, kārppu, kaippu, uvarppu, tuvarppu eṉa aṟuvakaic cuvai kaḷēyākum. iṅṅaṉam vakuttuk koṇṭa iyalpukaḷuḷ kaṭavuḷ enta niṟattiṉar, enta vaṭiviṉar, enta aḷaviṉar, enta cuvaiyiṉar eṉṟu evaraik kēṭpiṉum, avar iṉṉa niṟattiṉa rāyiruppar, iṉṉa vaṭiviṉarāyiruppar, iṉṉa aḷaviṉarā yiruppar, iṉṉa cuvaiyiṉarāyiruppar eṉa uṟutippaṭuttic colla māṭṭuvārallar. ākavē kaṭavuḷ poruḷkaḷiṉ ellaiyiṉaiyum iṭattiṉellaiyiṉaiyuṅ kālattiṉel laiyiṉaiyuṅ kaṭantu niṟpavarātalōṭu, avvap poruḷ kaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷilēyum aṭaṅkātavarāy avaṟṟai yuṅ kaṭantu niṟṟal ellārkkum otta karuttēyātal iṉitu viḷaṅkukiṉṟataṉṟō? iṅṅaṉamāka ellāvaṟṟaiyum ellā vaṟṟiṉiyalpukaḷaiyuṅ kaṭavuḷ kaṭantu niṟpavarāy uḷḷavar eṉpatu kaṭavuḷ eṉṉuñ coṟporuḷāl naṉku teḷiyak kiṭakkiṉṟamaiyiṉ, iv vuṇmaiyaip pallāyira āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉṉamē ārāyntuṇarnta nam paḻan tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ, nammā laṟiyappaṭṭa poruḷvakaikaḷilum uyirvakaikaḷilum av uyirkaḷiṉ tōṟṟa oṭukka vakai kaḷilum oṉṟāka ak kaṭavuḷaik kīḻk koṇarntu vaittuc collutaṟku oru ciṟitum maṉa micaiyātavarkaḷāy avaṉaip piṟavāṉ iṟavāṉ eṉṟē yāṇṭum valiyuṟuttic colvā rāyiṉar. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār, piṟappu iṟappukkaḷukku vittākiya viṉaiyiṉiṉṟum iṟaivaṉ nīṅki niṟpavaṉ eṉpatai
viṉaiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ muṉaivaṉ
eṉṟum, piṟappukku mutalvarāṉa tāyun tantaiyum illātavaṉ iṟaivaṉ eṉpatai māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ,
kōyil cuṭukāṭu kolpulittōl nallāṭai
  tāyumili tantaiyili tāṉtaṉiyaṉ kāṇēṭi
eṉṟun, tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉ,
venta cāmpal viraiyeṉap pūciyē
  tantai yāroṭu tāyilar tammaiyē
  cinti yāeḻu vārviṉai tīrpparāl
  entai yāravar evvakai yārkolō,
eṉṟuñ, camaṇcamaya muṉivarāṉa iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ,
piṟavā yākkaip periyōṉ kōyilum
eṉṟum aruḷic ceyvārāyiṉar. piṟkālattu vaiṇava camayattil uṟaittuniṉṟa villiputtūrāḻvāruṅ kūṭa vēṭṭu vaṉvaṭiyil vantu aruccuṉaṉoṭu pōrpurintu avaṉ kaivillāl aṭiyuṇṭa iṟaivaṉaip paṟṟi moḻiyum pōtu,
vētamaṭi yuṇṭaṉa virintapala ākama
	vitaṅkaḷaṭi yuṇṭaṉa ōraim
 pūtamaṭi yuṇṭaṉavi nāḻikaimu taṟpukalcey
	poḻutoṭu calippil poruḷiṉ
  pētamaṭi yuṇṭaṉa piṟappili iṟappili
	piṟaṅkal aracaṉṟaṉ makaḷār
  nātamama laṉcamara vēṭavaṭi vaṅkoṭu
	naraṉkaiyaṭi yuṇṭa poḻutē.
 eṉṟu iv uṇmaiyai oḷiyāmal naṉkeṭuttuk kūṟiṉār.

3. māṇikkavācakar aruḷicceyta kēṭṭārum aṟiyātāṉ 
eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷiṉ urai
ivvāṟellām iv ulakattiluḷḷa ellā makkaṭkum icaintatākiya kaṭavuḷiṉ potu ilakkaṇattaiyum, aṅṅaṉam evarkkum eṭṭātavarāy ellāmvalla iṟaivaṉ iruppiṉum empōṉṟa ciṟṟaṟiviṉārkkuk kāṇavuṅ karutavum paṭāta ak kaṭanta nilaiyilēyē avaṉ niṟpaṉāyiṉ avaṉatu tiruvaru ḷutaviyiṉṟi ippiṟavit tuṉpattai, ippiṟavi vittukku mūlamāṉa aṟiyāmaiyai yāmē nīkkik koḷḷa māṭṭāmaiyāl avaṉ tāyiṉum mikka irakkamum aṉpum vaittu emmaṉōrkku eḷivantu tōṉṟi aruḷ ceyyum ciṟappilakkaṇattaiyum oruṅku viḷakki nam caiva camaya mutalāciriyarāṉa māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ aruḷic ceytirukkum oru tiruppāṭṭiṉai īṇṭu ārāyntu pārppōmāka. at tiruppāṭṭu varumāṟu:
kēṭṭāru maṟiyātāṉ kēṭoṉ ṟillāṉ
	kiḷaiyilāṉ kēḷātē ellāṅ kēṭṭāṉ
  nāṭṭārkaḷ viḻittirukka ñālat tuḷḷē
	nāyiṉukkut taviciṭṭu nāyi ṉēṟkē
  kāṭṭā taṉavellāṅ kāṭṭip piṉṉuṅ
 	kēḷā taṉavelāṅ kēṭpit teṉṉai
  mīṭṭēyum piṟavāmaṟ kāttāṭ koṇṭāṉ
	emperumāṉ ceytiṭṭa viccai tāṉē.
arumaruntaṉṉa ic ceyyuḷiṉkaṇ nāmeṭuttuk koṇṭa ivvirivuraip poruḷiṉ mutaṟkaṇṇatākiya kaṭavuḷ nilaiyai māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ naṉku virittu viḷakkiyirukkiṉṟā rātaliṉ, icceyyuṭ coṟporuḷai ovvoṉṟāy ārāyntu pārppatu namakkup peritum payaṉpaṭumeṉak karuti avaṟṟai ārāyap pukukiṉṟām.
mutaliṟ kēṭṭāru maṟiyātāṉ eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭar aṟiviṟ ciṟanta ettakaiyiṉarai viṉaviyum iṟaivaṉ aṟiyappaṭātavaṉ eṉpataṉai viḷakkukiṉṟatu. iṟaivaṉiruk kum ūrētu, avaṉ pērētu, avaṉaip peṟṟār yār? ap peṟṟōriṉ peyar eṉṉai? eṉṟu nām evarai viṉāviṉālum avarellām avaṟṟiṟku viṭai colla māṭṭāmaiyiṉ, viṉāviyavar aṅṅaṉa mellām viṉāviyum avaṉai aṟintu koḷḷāta nilaiyilēyē irukkiṉṟaṉar. iṉṉun tavamuyaṟciyiṟ ṟērnta muṉivarkaḷuṅ kūṭa iṟaivaṉ iyalpukaḷaik kēṭṭaṟiyap pukuntu tamakkumēl niṟkum muṉivarkaḷaik kēṭka, avarkaḷum avaṟṟait tamakkut teriyātu eṉṟu tamakku mēṟpaṭṭa muṉivarkaḷaik kēṭka, avarun tamakkut teriyātu eṉṟu tamakku mēṟpaṭṭāraik kāṭṭik kaiviṭa, iṅṅaṉamē evar evaraik kēṭpiṉum avarellām avaṉṟaṉ muḻumutaṟṟaṉmaikaḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭa muṭiyāmal varunti niṟṟaliṉ kēṭṭāru maṟiyātāṉ eṉṟu mutaṟkiḷan tōtiṉār.
iṉik, kēṭoṉṟillāṉ eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭar iṟaivaṉ ekkālattum ettakaiya aḻivum ettakaiya ūṟu pāṭum illātavaṉ eṉpatai aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟatu. avaṉ oru kālattil illāmaliruntu piṉṉaiyoru kālattut tōṉṟiṉavaṉāy iruppiṉaṉṟō avvāṟu orukālattut tōṉṟiṉavaṉ piṉṉē oru kālattu iṟantu kēṭuṟuvā ṉeṉṟu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. kaṭavuḷukkut tāy tantai yāvār yāvareṉṟu evaraik kēṭpiṉum avarellām avaṟkut tāytantaiyar ilareṉṟē collutalāl, evarālum avaṉ piṟappaik kēṭṭaṟiya muṭiyāmaiyiṉ avaṉ piṟavātavaṉ eṉpatu teḷiyappaṭum. avvāṟu teḷiyap paṭavē avaṉukku iṟappum illaiyeṉpatu tāṉē pōtarum. evvevvuyir piṟakkiṉṟaṉavō avaiyellām iṟakkiṉṟaṉa. piṟavi yeṭutta uyirkaḷukkē iṟappum nikaḻak kāṇkiṉ ṟōmēyallatu piṟavātavaikaḷ iṟattalai yāṇṭuṅ kaṇṭilēm.
piṟappuṇṭēl iṟappuṇṭām, iṟappuṇṭēl piṟappuṇṭām eṉṉum mūtaṟiñar moḻiyum iv vuṇmai yiṉaiyē valiyuṟuttukiṉṟatu. piṟantiṟappaṉavellāñ ciṟṟaṟivuñ ciṟu toḻilumuṭaiya uyirkaḷeṉṟum, piṟantiṟa vātavaṉ oruvaṉē iṟaivaṉ eṉṟum pakuttuṇarntu koḷvatu mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātatākum. ivvāṟu uyir kaḷukkum iṟaivaṉukkum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭṭiṉaiyum av vēṟupāṭṭukku ētuvāṉa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷaiyuṅ kaṭaip piṭiyāka uṇarntu koḷḷavēṇṭuvatu perumpayaṉ peṟa vēṇṭum makkaḷiṉ meyyuṇarccikku mutaṉmaiyākum. ivvēṟupāṭṭiṉaik, kaṭavuḷukkuriya piṟantiṟavāc ciṟappilak kaṇattai mutaṉmutaṟ kaṇṭaṟintavarkaḷ caivacamaya naṉmakkaḷē yāvar. paḻaiya yūtarkaḷum, makamatiyarkaḷun tavira maṟṟac camayattavarkaḷellārum piṟantiṟakkum uyirkaḷaiyē avvavarpāṟ kāṇappaṭṭa cila āṇmaic ceyalkaḷāṟ kaṭavu ḷākap piṟaḻa uṇarntu avai tammaiyē kaṭavuḷ nilaiyil vaittu vaṇaṅki varukiṉṟārkaḷ. āṉāl it tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ kaṇ uḷḷa caivacamayattavarkaṇ maṭṭum paṇṭaikkālan toṭṭup piṟantiṟavāt taṉmaiyaṉē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāvaṉ eṉat teḷiya uṇarntu iṉṟukāṟum ak koḷkaiyiṟ ciṟitum vaḻuvātavarkaḷāy nilaipēṟuṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉar. piṟappiṟappillā ik kaṭavuḷilakkaṇattai nam caivacamaya āciriyarum piṟarum valiyuṟuttip pāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷai muṉṉarē nuṅkaṭku eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉēm. iṉṉum nam cantāṉāciriyarākiya aruṇanti civaṉārum,
yātoru teyvaṅ koṇṭīr atteyva māki āṅkē
  mātoru pāka ṉārtām varuvarmaṟ ṟattey vaṅkaḷ
  vētaṉaippaṭum piṟakkum iṟakkum mēlviṉaiyuñ ceyyum
  ātalāl ivaii lātāṉ aṟintuaruḷ ceyva ṉaṉṟē
eṉṟu varaiyaṟuttuk kūṟiya arumait tiruppāṭṭum nammaṉōr ovvoruvar uḷḷattiluṅ kaṉmēṟcetukkiya eḻuttuppōl nilaipeṟṟu niṉṟu niṉaivukūraṟ pālatākum.
aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, muṉṉē yūtarkaḷum makamatiyar kaḷuṅkūṭak kaṭavuḷukkup piṟappiṟappuc collavillai yeṉṟamaiyāṟ caiva naṉmakkaḷē ak kaṭavuḷilakkaṇattai aṟintārkaḷeṉpatu yāṅṅaṉameṉiṉ, avarkaḷ kaṭavuḷukkup piṟappiṟappuc collāviṭṭāluṅ kaṭavuḷ piṟantiṟavātavaṉ eṉpataṉaiyum uyirkaḷē piṟantiṟappaṉa eṉpataṉaiyuñ caivacamaya āciriyarkaḷaip pōlap pirittuk kāṭṭi avarkaḷ āciriyaṉmār evarun tamatu viviliya vētattilātal kurāṉi lātal valiyuṟuttic colli viḷakkiṉārillai. maṟṟut tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ nūlkaḷilō mikap paḻaiyakālan toṭṭē kaṭavuḷ viṉaiyiṉ nīṅkiya vaṉeṉṟum, avaṉ piṟavā yākkaiya ṉeṉṟun, tantai tāy illātavaṉeṉṟum, ellā uyirkaḷum iṟanta cuṭukāṭṭil tāṉ eṉṟum iṟavātu niṉṟē āṭuvāṉeṉṟum palakālum vaṟpuṟuttic collum meyyu raikaḷ aṭuttaṭuttuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ēṉaic camayattavar nūlkaḷilō iṅṅaṉam varaiyaṟutturaikkum meyyurai yiṉaik kāṇṭaliyalātu. iṟaivaṉilakkaṇattait teḷiya uṇartalum iyalātu. ākavē, piṟappu vaḷarppukaḷ oruvāṟṟā ṉuṅ kēṭṭaṟiyappaṭāta mutalvaṉukku evvakai yāṉa kēṭum ekkālattum evviṭattum nikaḻāteṉpatu terittaṟ poruṭ ṭākavē kēṭoṉṟillāṉ eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭaraik kēṭṭāru maṟiyātāṉ eṉṉuñ coṟ ṟoṭarkkup piṉṉē peritum iyaipupaṭavaittu māṇikka vācakap perumāṉ aruḷic ceyvārāyiṉar.
iṉik, kiḷaiyilāṉ eṉpatu cuṟṟattārai illāta vaṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. uṟaviṉaraik kuṟikkuñ cuṟṟam kēḷ uṟavu mutalāṉa pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷirukkavum avaṟṟai yellām viṭṭuk kiḷaiyeṉṉun tamiḻc collāl aṭikaḷ ataṉai aruḷic ceytirukkum nuṭpam ārāyaṟpālatu. kiḷai yeṉṉuñcol oruvarkkum avarōṭu uṭampiṉ toṭarpu uṭaiyārkkum uḷḷa neruṅkiya icaiviṉait terivikku māṟupōl maṟṟac coṟkaḷ terivikkavillai. kiḷai yeṉpatu mutaṉ mutal marattiṉ kōṭukaḷukkē peyarāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatākum. oru maramum am marattiṉiṉṟu pala kavarkaḷākap piriyuṅ kiḷaikaḷum oṉṟaiviṭṭoṉṟu vēṟākāmaṟ peritum iyaintu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉamē oruvaṉukkuk kiḷaikaḷ pōl neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiyarātaṟ kuriyār; avaṉ tāṉ piṟattaṟku iṭamāṉa tāy tantaiyarun, taṉṉōṭuṭaṉ piṟanta tamaiyaṉ tampi mutaliyavarun, tammiṉiṉṟu piṟakkum makkaḷum, am makkaḷiṉiṉṟu piṟakkum makkaḷun, taṉṉōṭuṭaṉ piṟantāriṉiṉṟu piṟakkum makkaḷum, am makkaḷukku makkaḷum eṉa avaṉōṭu neruṅkiya uṟavu vāyntārē yāvar. maṟṟac cuṟṟattār uṟaviṉar kēḷir eṉpavarellām avaṉukku attuṇai nerukkam uṭaiyā rallar. atupaṟṟiyē tāṉ oruvaṉukku mika neruṅkiya uṭampiṉ toṭarpuṭaiyāraik kuṟittaṟku māṇikka vācakap perumāṉ kiḷaiyeṉṉuñ collaic ciṟanta toṉṟāka eṭuttuk koṇṭār.
iṉi, iṟaivaṉ tāṉ piṟattaṟku iṭaṉāṉa tāy tantaiyarun, taṉṉō ṭuṭaṉpiṟantārun, taṉakku makkaḷā vārum, am makkaḷukku makkaḷāvārun, taṉṉuṭaṉ piṟan tārkku makkaḷāvārum, am makkaḷukkup putalvarāvārum illātava ṉeṉpatu muṉṉamē peṟappaṭṭamaiyāl, attakaiya neruṅkiya cuṟṟat toṭarpu ciṟitumillātavaṉ eṉpatai aṟivittaṟkē kiḷaiyilāṉ eṉṟār. kiḷaiñarākiya peruntoṭarpu illātavarkaḷukku at toṭarpu paṟṟi varum maṟṟaic cuṟṟattārum illāmai tāṉē peṟap paṭumātalāl, iṟaivaṉukku vēṟu akaṉṟa cuṟṟattārum illai eṉpatu itu koṇṭu muṭikkappaṭum. neruṅkiya uṭampiṉ toṭarpi liruntē ēṉai ellāc cuṟṟaṅkaḷum ac cuṟṟaṅkaḷaip paṟṟi varum palvēṟu allalkaḷum oṉṟilirun toṉṟāyk kiḷaikkak kāṇṭalāl, av vellāvaṟṟiṟkum aṭippaṭaiyāṉa uṭampiṉ toṭarpiṉai iṟaivaṉ oru ciṟitu millātavaṉ eṉpatu terittaṟkē uṭampiṉ toṭarpu kkup peyarāṉa kiḷaiyeṉṉuñ collai eṭuttāṇṭu ac collāl evvakait toṭakkumillāta iṟaivaṉiyalpaic curuṅkac collal āḻamuṭaittātal eṉṉum aḻaku peṟak kūṟiya aṭikaḷiṉaṟiviṉ tiṟattai eṉṉeṉpēṉ!
aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, eṇṇiṟanta uyirkaḷ iṟaivaṉ eṉṟuḷḷāṉō aṉṟutoṭṭē avaṉiṭattil tāmum tammiṭattil avaṉumāka neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiyarāy iruttalāl uyirkaḷai avaṉukkuk kiḷaiñarākak kūṟutal poruttamaṉṟō eṉiṟ, kiḷaiñareṉpār oruvaruk koruvar utaviyun tuṇaiyumāy irukkun taṉmaiyarāvar. itu kiḷaiyeṉṉuñ colliṉ mutaṟporuḷiṉālēyē iṉitu viḷaṅkā niṟkum. eṅṅaṉameṉiṉ, marattāṟ kiḷaiyuṅ kiḷaiyāl maramum uyirvāḻkiṉṟaṉa. aṭimarattai veṭṭi ṉāṟ kiḷaikaḷ paṭṭuppōm. kiḷaikaḷaiyellām veṭṭi ṉāl maramum paṭṭuppōm. itu perumpālum ellā maraṅ kaḷiṭattuṅ kāṇappaṭum uṇmai nikaḻcciyām. itupōlavē tāytantaiyar tam makkaḷ iḷaiñarāyirukkum poḻutu avarkku utaviyun tuṇaiyumāy niṉṟu avaraip pātukāttu uyirvāḻac ceykiṉṟaṉar. makkaḷ vaḷarntu periyarām poḻutu tam tāytantaiyar āṇṭiṉ mutirntu valivukuṉṟap peṟutalāl makkaḷ avarkku utaviyun tuṇaiyumāy niṉṟu avarai uyirvāḻac ceykiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉamē uṭaṉ piṟantārum oruvarukkoruvar utaviyun tuṇaiyumāy 
niṉṟu uyirvāḻtalai nērē kāṇkiṉṟōm. ākavē, kiḷaiñarāvā rellārum oruvarukkoruvar utaviyun tuṇaiyumāy niṉṟu oḻukiṉāl maṭṭum avar kiḷaiñareṉṟu peyar peṟutaṟ kuriyāreṉpatu peṟappaṭum. inta muṟaiyil vaittuk kaṭavuḷuṅ kaṭavuḷoṭu toṉṟu toṭṭu irukkum uyir kaḷum oruvarukkoruvar utaviyuṅ tuṇaiyumāyiruntu uyir vāḻun taṉmaiyarāyiṉ, avar tammaiyuṅ kiḷaiñareṉṟu moḻital poruntum. kaṭavuḷ uyirkaḷukku uṟṟa tuṇaiyum utaviyumāy niṟṟal pōla uyirkaḷuṅ kaṭavuḷukku uṟṟa tuṇaiyum utaviyumāy niṉṟu avarai uyir vāḻac ceyku varāyiṉ, avarai avarkkuk kiḷaiñareṉṟu kūṟutal poruttamām. maṟṟu uyirkaḷō paṇṭu toṭṭē aṟiyāmai yeṉṉum pēriruḷāl viḻuṅkap paṭṭut tammaiyum uṇarāmal tam uyirkkuyirāy niṉṟu tamakkup pērutavi ceytuvarum mutalvaṉaiyum uṇarāmal valiyaṟṟa nilaiyiṉarāy irutta lālum, iṟai vaṉuṭaiya utaviyaip peṟṟu aṟivu viḷaṅkit tammaiyun talaivaṉaiyum uṇarumnilai vanta ñāṉṟun tammuṭaiya aṟivuñ ceyalum iṟaivaṉṟaṉ pēraṟivu pērāṟṟalkaḷiṉ muṉ valiviḻantaṉa vāyē iruppatallatu av viṟaivaṟkut tam aṟivālun tam ceyalāluñ ceyyattakka utavi ciṟitu millāmaiyālum, ellāc celvamuñ ceyalum uṭaiya iṟaivaṉukkumuṉ av uyirkaḷ, ceyaliḻanta vaṟiya iravalarē yāvarallatu avaṉukkuk kiḷaiñarātal oruvāṟṟā ṉuñ cellātu. iṅṅaṉam eṇṇiṟanta av uyirt tokai kaḷai yellām ellāmvalla iṟaivaṉukkuk kiḷaiñarākac collutal oruvāṟṟāṉum icaiyāmaiyāl, iv uṇmaiyai naṉkuṇarnta māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ kiḷaiyilāṉ eṉṟu aruḷicceytāreṉpatu.
iṉik, kēḷātē ellāṅ kēṭṭāṉ eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭar piṟar colvaṉavaṟṟaic ceviyeṉṉuṅ karuviyāṟ kēṭṭu aṟiyum nammaṉōr pōlātu, ak karuviyillāmalē ulakiṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa ellā makkaḷuñ colluñ coṟkaḷaiyum niṉaikkum niṉaivukaḷaiyum aṟiya vallāṉ eṉavum, vāyeṉṉuṅ karuvi koṇṭu piṟarpāl aṟiya vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟaik kēṭṭaṟiyum nammaṉōr pōlātu ak karuviyiṉ utaviyillāmalē piṟar uḷḷattu nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷaiyellān taṉ niṉaiviṉālēyē uṇara vallāṉ eṉavum iruporuḷ taruvatām. makkaṭpiṟavi yiṉarākiya nām ceviyiṉutaviyiṉṟip piṟar colvaṉavaṟṟaik kēṭṭuṇarum āṟṟalillātavarkaḷāyirukkiṉṟōm. vāyiṉu taviyiṉṟip piṟarai viṉāvit teriya vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟait terintu koḷḷa iyalātavarkaḷāyirukkiṉṟōm. ceviyum vāyu millāta ceviṭarum ūmarum ak karuvikaḷ illāmaiyāṟ paṭun tuṉpattaik kaṇṭu evvaḷavu irakkamuṟu kiṉṟōm. tammiṉum aṟiviṟ ciṟantavarkaḷillaiyeṉavun, tammiṉum valimaiyiṟ ciṟantavarkaḷillaiyeṉavum iṟumāntu niṉṟōr kuruṭar ūmar mutalāṉa uṟuppaṟaikaḷaik kaṇṭu tāmum avarkaḷaip pōṟ paḻutupaṭṭiruntāl tammuṭaiya nilaiyum evvaḷavu tuṉpuṟat takkatā yirukkumeṉṟu niṉaintu ataṉāl tamatu ciṟumaiyum ellāmvalla iṟaivaṉṟaṉ perumaiyum uṇarntu neñcam nekkurukutalaik kāṇkiṉṟōmallamō? iṅṅaṉamē ivvariya uṭampil amainta maṟṟaip poṟikaḷum maṟṟai uṟuppukkaḷum paḻutupaṭṭup pōkumāṉāl, avvuṭam piṉkaṇ niṟku muyir ataṉāl aṟivu viḷaṅkap peṟātu ētoru ceyalumiṉṟi veṟuṅkallaip pōṟ kiṭakkumaṉṟō? iṟaivaṉ aruḷkaṉintu aḷittaruḷiya ivvuṟuppukaḷiṉ utaviyai muḻutum peṟṟu uyir vāḻuñ cilar avvutaviyait tiṉaittuṇaiyum niṉaintupārāmal yāmē kaṭavuḷeṉṟu cerukkip pēcā niṟpar. avaruṭaiya cerukkellām avartam uṟuppukaḷ cīrkulaintu pōṉāl nilaikkumō coṉ miṉkaḷ aṉparkaḷē! 
ivvāṟāka uṭampiṉutaviyum uṭampilamainta uṟuppu kaḷiṉutaviyum iṉṟi aṟivuñ ceyaluṅ kaikūṭap peṟāta nammaṉōr nilaimaikkum, ivvuṟuppukaḷiṉ utavi ciṟitum vēṇṭāmalē eṇṇiṟanta ulakaṅkaḷaiyum avvulakaṅkaḷi luḷḷa eṇṇiṟanta kōṭi uyirkaḷaiyuṅ kāttum paṭaittuṅ karantum viḷaiyāṭi varum ellām valla aiyaṉuṭaiya nilai maikkum oppumai eṅkē! toṉṟu toṭṭē pēriruḷākiya aṟiyāmaiyiṟ kiṭantu aṟivoḷi maḻuṅkip piṉṉar iṟaivaṉ tanta uṭampālum avvuṭampiṉ uṟuppukaḷāluñ ciṟituciṟitāka aṟivu viḷaṅkap peṟṟu varum nammaṉōrkkē ūṉuṭampiṉ karuvikaḷ vēṇṭappaṭuvataṉṟi, ik karuvikaḷaip paṭaittuk koṭukkum ellāmvalla iṟaivaṉukku ik karuvikaḷum ivaṟṟiṉutavikaḷuñ ciṟitum vēṇṭappaṭā; itu paṟṟiyē,
ceviyiṉāṟ kēḷātatāyc ceviyiṉaik kēṭpippatāy uḷḷatetuvō atuvē piramam eṉṟum, vāyiṉāṟ pēcātatāy vāyiṉaip pēcuvippatāy uḷḷa tetuvō atuvē piramam eṉṟum aṟi; ataṉ pakkattēyiruntu ataṉāl iyakkappaṭukiṉṟa nī piramamākāy.
eṉak kēnōpaniṭatam vaṟpuṟuttuk kūṟuvatāyiṟṟu.. ivvuṇmaiyiṉaiyē māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ, kēḷātē ellāṅ kēṭṭāṉ eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭarāl teḷiyak kūṟiṉār.
eṉṟu itukāṟuṅ kēṭṭāru maṟiyātāṉ, kēṭoṉṟil lāṉ, kiḷaiyilāṉ, kēḷātē yellāṅ kēṭṭāṉ eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṟ peṟappaṭṭa kaṭavuḷ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ avvaḷa vum in nilavulakattuḷḷa ellā makkaḷālum ellāc camayattārālum ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭuvaṉavēyākum. ākavē ellārkkum uṭaṉpāṭāṉa ivai kaṭavuḷukkuriya potu ilakkaṇaṅka ḷeṉappaṭum. ippotu ilakkaṇaṅkaḷāl, ip potu vilakkaṇaṅkaḷai uṭaiyavarāka nammāl ārāyntaṟiyap paṭṭa kaṭavuḷnilai, ciṟṟaṟiviṉarākiya namatu collukkum niṉaivukkum eṭṭātatāyirukkiṉṟatu. epporuḷuṅ kaṭanta mutalvaṉai, evarāluṅ kēṭṭaṟiyappaṭāta talaivaṉai, evvakai aḻivukkum akappaṭāta āṇṭavaṉai, evvakaip paṭṭa kiḷaiñarumillāta taṉṉan taṉiyaṉai, yāmaṟiyātē yāmkēḷātē irukkavum emakku vēṇṭuvaṉavellān tantu talaiyaḷittuvarun tōṉṟāt tuṇaiyai ēḻaiyēm eṅṅaṉaṅ kāṇpēm? mtdJ mU£nguÄœj¤ij v§‡d« gUFnt«?emmai iṭaiyaṟātu paṟṟit toṭarntuvarum piṟavit tuṉpattai eṅṅaṉam nīkkuvēm? eṉṟu eṇṇi eṇṇi neñcaṅ kuḻaiyāniṉṟa nēraṅkaḷil avaṉatu eṭṭā nilaimai yil em maṉam paṟṟuvatiṉṟāy avaṉ tiruvuruviṉaik kāṇa vum avaṉ tiruvaṭikaḷaip paṟṟik koṇṭu tēmpavum viḻaintu eṉpillāp puḻuviṉuṅ kaṭaippaṭṭa emmaiyum kaṭaikkaṇippaṉō eṉṟu ematuḷḷam ēṅkukiṉṟataṉṟō? attakaiya aṭaṅkā ēkkattiṉpāṟpaṭṭa em ciṟṟuyirkkuk kaṭavuḷiṉ eṭṭā nilaimaiyaic collic collik kāṭṭuvatāl em ēkkaṅ tīrumō? tīrātaṉṟē! avaṉ, taṉṉil evvaḷavu periya ṉāyiṉum evvaḷavu eṭṭā nilaimai yaṉāyiṉum em pōṉṟa ciṟṟuyirkaḷukku iraṅki eḷivantu tōṉṟi aruḷ ceyyiṉaṉṟō ematu ēkkan tīrum, appōtaṉṟō ema tuḷḷam ivvulakattai maṟantu, iṅkuḷḷa toṭarpukaḷai maṟantu mēleḻumpi avaṉ tiruvaṭikaḷiṟ paṭiyum; appōtaṉ ṟō kaṭavuḷiṉ uṇmai nilai emakku viḷaṅkum. atukāṟuñ carukkaraiyaik kaṇṭaṟiyātavaṉ, ataṉ cuvaiyaic cuvaittaṟiyātavaṉ ataṉ iṉimaiyiṉai viyantu viyantu pēcic calippaṭaivatallatu ataṉaip peṟṟuc cuvaittu makiḻumvakai avaṟkuk kaikūṭātavāṟu pōl, yāmum kaṭavuḷiṉ eṭṭāta nilaikaḷai, avaṉṟaṉ pēriṉpap peruñcuvaiyaip pēcippēci eṇṇi eṇṇi vāṭuvatallatu avaṉaip peṟṟu avaṉatu pēriṉpavayamāy niṟkum perumpēṟu emakkuk kaikūṭumō?
kaṭavuḷ tāmē tōṉṟiyava reṉpatum, avaratu iruppu iṉṟiyamaiyāta teṉpatum, māyaiyaik kaṭantavareṉpatum, avar eḷiyar eṉpatum, ciṟṟuyirkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum varicaikkum uḷvēṟṟumaikkum avar mēmpaṭṭava reṉpatum, pakukkappaṭātava reṉpatum, uyiruḷ poruḷ kaḷilum iyakkaṅkaḷilum iṉṟiyamaiyāt taṉmaikaḷilum avaiyallāta taṉmaikaḷilum maṟaintuḷḷa ceyalkaḷilum veḷippaṭṭa ceyalkaḷilum uḷḷa vēṟṟumaikaḷ avar uṭaiyarallar eṉpatum, avar ōriṉattiṟ cērkkappaṭutalil lātava reṉpatum, meymmai vāynta aḷavilāt  taṉmaiya reṉpatun, tām kāṭṭum nalliyalpukaḷukku vēṟāṉa tiruvuruvam vāyntavareṉpatun, tīmaiyiṉ iruppukku iṭantaruvarē yaṉṟi at tīmaiyiṉai uṇṭu paṇṇupavar allareṉpatun, tāmē ellā muṭaiyareṉpatun, tampā laṉpiṉar eṉpatun, tampāl taṉitta pēriṉpattai uṭaiya reṉpatum ākiya avaruṭaiya iyalpukaḷ ematu vāḻkkai yōṭu ev vakaiyāṉa toṭarpu koṇṭu niṟkiṉṟaṉa? avaruṭaiya ivviyalpukaḷ taṉittaṉiyē tamakku otta vakaiyāka ematu oḻukalāṟṟiṉait tiruttik koḷvataṟku iṭañ ceyyāvāyiṉ, avai uṇmaiyē yāyiṉum alla tavai poyyēyāyiṉum avai oruvaṉuṭaiya camaya vāḻk kaikku entavakaiyil vēṟupaṭṭa payaṉait tarakkūṭum?
eṉṉaḷavil mēṟkuṟikkappaṭṭa kaṭavuḷ iyalpukaḷaip paṟṟiya niṉaivukaḷukku māṟāṉavaikaḷaic colvatil yāṉ veṟuppuṭaiyēṉāyiṉum, ivaikaḷ kuṟṟamillātaṉavākavē eṭukkappaṭumāyiṉum namatu camaya vāḻkkaikku ivai tiṉait tuṇaiyāvatu payaṉpaṭumeṉṟu karutakkūṭavillai eṉpatait tiṟantu colkiṉṟēṉ. ākavē, ivai oṉṟum uṇmai yāvaṉa alla. kaṭavuḷ eḷimaiyuṭaiyarāyiruntāl anta iyalpukkup porutta māmpaṭi yāṉ evvakaiyāṉa oḻuka lāṟṟil oḻukakkūṭum, ataṉai coṉmiṉkaḷ! avar taṉip paṭṭa niṟaivāṉa iṉpamuṭaiyarāṉāl, ataṉai uṇarvata ṉāl yāṉ eṉatu naṭakkaiyai evvāṟu vakuttuk koḷḷak kūṭumō ataṉaic coṉmiṉkaḷ! attakaiya iyalpukaḷaip paṟṟiya coṟṟokutikaḷ kaṭavuḷaip paṟṟiya uṇmaiyāṉa uṇarcciyai emakkut tarukiṉṟaṉavā? kaṭavuḷiruppiṉai nampiya kōṭpāṭukaḷ toṭarpāka nilavu māyiṉum, uṇmaic camayamāṉatu in nilavulakattil illāmaṟ paṟantōṭi viṭum. āṉāṟ, payaṉpaṭattakka camaya vāḻkkai yiliruntu nōkku vēṉāṉāl, mēṟkuṟitta veṟṟiyalpukaḷ kalvip pulamai mikkōr kaṭṭiviṭṭa ciṟitum payaṉpaṭāta kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷē yallāmal avaṟṟai vēṟeṉ ṉeṉṟu viḷampuvēṉ!
iṉi, avai aṅṅaṉamirukka namatu oḻukkamuṟaikku otta kaṭavuḷiṉ iyalpukaḷaic ciṟitu ārāyvām. ivviyalpu kaḷō accattaiyum nampikkaiyiṉaiyum nilaipeṟac ceytu tūya tuṟavu vāḻkkaikku aṭippaṭai kōlukiṉṟaṉa. yāṅṅaṉa meṉiṟ, kaṭavuḷ tūyataṉmai yuṭaiyareṉṟu koṇṭāl, avar uyirkaḷiṉ naṉmaiyiṉaiyaṉṟi vēṟoṉṟaṉaiyuṅ karutār. avar ellām vallareṉṟu koṇṭāl avar an naṉmaiyiṉai uyirkaḷukku muṭittuttara vallunarāvar. avar ellām uṇarpavar eṉṟu koṇṭāl avar iruḷilum nammaik kāṇa vallunarāvar. avar naṭunilaiyāḷareṉṟu koṇṭāl nammuṭaiya tavaṟukaḷaik kaṇṭu avar oṟukka vallunarāvar. avar aṉpuṭaiyareṉak koṇṭāl nam iḻukkaṅkaḷai avar maṉṉikkavum vallunarāvar. avar māṟāta iyaṟkaiyareṉṟu koṇṭāl avaratu utaviyil nām nampiyirukkak kūṭum. ittakaiya iyalpukaḷē namatu vāḻkkaiyōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiyaṉavāka irutta lāl, ivaikaḷaip paṟṟi nām terintu koḷvatu mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātatākum. kaṭavuḷ ulakattaip paṭaittataṉ nōkkam avar tamatu pēraruṭ ciṟappiṉai uyirkaḷukku vaḻaṅka vēṇṭu meṉpatē yākaiyāl ivviyalpum namatu vāḻkkai yōṭu tiṭṭamāṉa toṭarpuṭaiyatāka viḷaṅkāniṟkiṉṟatu1 eṉavum, kaṭanta nilaiyiṉaik kūṟum māyāvāta ārāycci yuraikaḷ camaya uṇmaiyiṉai ulakamellān taḻuvumpaṭi ceyya māṭṭātavaṉavāy vaḻuvukiṉṟaṉa2 eṉavum amerik kāvil meyyuṇarvi ṉarāy viḷaṅkiya u.villiyam jēm eṉṉuñ cāṉṟōr āḻntu ārāyntu uraitta aṟivuraikaḷ, kaṭavuḷiṉ potuviyal pākiya kaṭanta nilaimaiyiṉai vaṟpuṟuttu uraikkuṅ kōṭpāṭukaḷ ciṟṟuyirkaḷākiya namakku evvāṟṟāṉum payaṉpaṭā eṉpataiyum, avar ciṟṟuyirka ḷellāvaṟṟiṟkum vēṇṭuvatākiya aḻiyāp perum pēṟṟai nalkutaṟkum ataṉai nalkutaṟporuṭṭu eḷiyarāy vantu avai tammai āṭ koḷḷutaṟkum avarkku iyaṟkaiyāyuḷḷa aṉpum aruḷum irakkamumākiya arum pēriyalpukaḷē emakku evvāṟṟāṉum payaṉpaṭumeṉpa taiyum iṉitu viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa.
kaṭanta nilaimaiyaṉāy niṟkum iṟaivaṉaic ciṟṟuyir kaḷākiya emmuṭaṉ neruṅkat toṭarpu paṭuttuvaṉa, aṉpum aruḷum irakkamumākiya ivviyalpukaḷēyām. ciṟṟuyir kaḷākiya ematu iv vulaka vāḻkkaiyi ṉuḷḷum oruvarōṭu oruvarai maṉamakiḻa icaivittu uyirvāḻac ceyvatum aṉpum aruḷum irakkamum allavō? aṉpillātavarkaḷ, piṟa uyirkaḷiṉ tuṉpattaik kaṇṭu iraṅkātavarkaḷ oruvarō ṭoruvar kūṭi uyirvāḻtalum utavi vēṇṭum uyirkaḷukku utavipuritalum ulakattil nikaḻak kaṇṭatuṇṭō coṉ miṉkaḷ! itu paṟṟiyaṉṟō āciriyar teyvap pulamait tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār,
aṉpilār ellān tamakkuriyar aṉpuṭaiyār
  eṉpu muriyar piṟarkku.
eṉṟu aruḷic ceyvā rāyiṉar. tāṉīṉṟa kuḻantai nōy vāyppaṭṭu varuntukaiyil ūṇum uṟakkamumiṉṟi ataṉ pakkattē iruntu tāyāṉavaḷ kavalai koṇṭu varuntumāṟu ceyvatu etu? pōrmēṟ ceṉṟa taṉ kaṇavaṉ iṟantāṉ eṉak kēṭṭu ēṅki uyirtuṟanta kātaliyai aṅṅaṉam uyir tuṟakku māṟu ceytatu etu? tāṉ vaḻipaṭukaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvuruvak kaṇṇiṉiṉṟu cennīr vaṭiya nōkkiya āṟṟātu tam kaṇṇai iṭantu ak kaṭavuḷ vaṭivattiṉ kaṇṇil appiya kaṇṇap  parai aṅṅaṉam appumāṟu ceytatu etu? īṉṟu aṇiya paṉṟik kuṭṭikaḷ tam tāyiṉai iḻantu nilaikkaḷaṉ kāṇātu tattaḷittalaik kaṇṭu tāṅkāmal tāyppaṉṟi vaṭiveṭuttu avaṟṟiṉ tuṉpattaip pōkkiya iṟaivaṉai aṅṅaṉam ceyyu māṟu ēviyatu ētu? vēṭaṉāl turattap paṭṭut taṉṉakam pukunta puṟāviṉ allalkaṇṭu, appuṟāviṉ eṭaikku īṭākat taṉṉu ṭampiṉ cataiyiṉai aṟuttuk koṭutta cōḻa maṉṉaṉai aṅṅaṉañ ceyyumāṟu tūṇṭiyatu etu? eṉṟu naṉku ārāyntu uṇariṉ aṉpum aruḷum irakkamumē avvā ṟellām ceyvavāyiṉa eṉpatu pulappaṭukiṉṟataṉṟō?
ākavē ciṟṟuyirkaḷukkuc, ciṟṟuyirkaḷiṉ aṟivukku, niṉaivukku eṭṭāta nilaiyil niṟkuṅ kaṭavuḷai avvuyir kaḷiṉ aṟivukkum niṉaivukkuṅ kāṭcikkuṅkūṭa eṭṭiya nilaiyilē eḷitākak  koṇṭu vantu kāṭṭavallatu kaṭavuḷi ṭattuḷḷa aruṭṭaṉmaiyē eṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅkaṟ pālatām. kaṭavuḷ maṟṟai ellā uyarnta taṉmaikaḷum uṭaiyarā yiruppiṉum avvaruṭṭaṉmai oṉṟu maṭṭum  ilarāyiṉ ivvulakattaiyum ivvulakattuṟṟa uyirkaḷaiyum nām kāṇṭaliyalātu. piṟarpaṭun tuṉpattaik kaṇṭu irakkappaṭāta oruvar avarkaṭku ētōr utaviyuñ ceyyak kaṇṭatuṇ ṭō? irakkamum īra neñcamum illātavarkaḷ piṟarōṭu aṉpāyp pēciṉum eṅkē avarkku utavi ceyya nērntu viṭumō eṉṟu neñcam naṭunaṭuṅkip piṟaroṭu pēcātum avarōṭu aḷavaḷāvātun taṉittuṟavu nilaiyai aṭai talaik kaṇṭāmaṉṟō? ittakaiya tuṟavikaḷuḷḷē kaṭavuḷum oruvarāy viṭuvarāyiṉ, ematu uyirvāḻkkai ivvulakattil naṭaipeṟumeṉṟu kaṉavilum niṉaital kūṭumō? ātaliṉā laṉṟō, nam caivacamayat teyvamākiya civapirāṉ taṉṉai ittakaiya tuṟavunilaiyiṟ cērttuk koḷḷātu kaṅkaiyum umaiyumākiya irumaṉaiviyaraiyum, piḷḷaiyār muruka ṉeṉṉum, iraṇṭu makkaḷaiyum uṭaiyaṉāyt taṉakku aṉpē vaṭiveṉpatai uṇarttalā yiṉāṉ. ivvarum pēruṇmai maṟṟōriṭattum nam māṇikka vācakap perumā ṉāl,
teṉpāl ukantāṭum tillaicciṟ ṟampalavaṉ
  peṇpāl ukantāṉ perumpittaṉ kāṇēṭī
  peṇpāl ukantilaṉēṟ pētāy irunilattōr
  viṇpāli yōkeyti viṭuvarkāṇ cāḻalō.
eṉṟu aṟivuṟuttappaṭṭatu.
aṉparkaḷē! iṅkē yām kūṟiyatu koṇṭu ellām valla mutalvaṉukku maṉaivimārum makkaḷum uḷarpōlum eṉa niṉaintu namatu muḻumutaṟ ṟeyvattai iḻivāka niṉaintu viṭēṉmiṉ! maṉaivi makkaḷeṉṟa toṭarpellām aṉpiṉaip pulappaṭuttum aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷēyām. aṉpum iṉpamumākiya taṉṉiyalpukaḷ taṉṉōṭu toṭarpuṭaiyāri ṭattup pulaṉāvaṉavaṉṟi vēṟu tāmākavē pulaṉāvaṉavalla. iṟaivaṉuṭaiya aṉpaiyum iṉpattaiyum peṟutaṟku urimai uṭaiyarāy avaṉai neruṅki niṟkum nāmellām avaṉukku maṉaiviyarum makkaḷumēyāvam. maṉaivi makkaḷeṉṟa toṭar pukaḷiṉ uṇmaiyai āḻntu ārāyumiṭattu avai aṉpeṉ ṉum vitaiyil muḷaitteḻuntu ōṅkip paṭarnta tēmāviṉ tēṉoḻuku kaṉikaḷēyām. iṉṉum itaṉai nuṇuki nōkku miṭattu ulakameṅkaṇum pulaṉākātu maṟaintu niṟkum oḷi yiṉaiyuṅ kata katappiṉaiyum pulaṉākak kāṭṭun tiṅkaḷum ñāyiṟumē at toṭarpukaḷām eṉṟu karututalum iḻuk kātu. inta muṟaiyilētāṉ kaṭavuḷukkuñ ciṟṟuyir kaḷākiya emakkum uḷḷa toṭarpu karutaṟpāṟ ṟeṉpataṟkē, civa perumāṉukku maṉaiviyarum makkaḷum uḷarāka vaittup puṉainturai vakaiyāl āṉṟōr kūṟuvārāyiṉar. itu koṇṭu kaṭavuḷ tām eṭṭā nilaiyiṉarāyiṉun, tām an nilaiyilēyē irukkum viruppu ilarāy, emakkun tamatu pēriṉpattai vaḻaṅkutaṟ poruṭṭu eḻunta pēr aruṭṭaṉ maiyāl emakku eḷiyarāy emakku aṇukkarāy yām vēṇṭiyavāṟellām vantu utavi ceyyuñ ciṟappiyalpu vāyntavareṉpatē aṟital vēṇṭum. itu veṟuñ collaḷavil nillātu uṇmaiyāka nikaḻvatātalai, māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ tamakku iṟavaṉ kaṭpulaṉāka eḻuntaruḷi vantu aruḷ ceyta uṇmai aruḷ nikaḻcciyai ōreṭuttuk kāṭṭāyk kūṟuvāṉ pukuntu,
nāṭṭārkaḷ viḻittirukka ñālat tuḷḷē
	nāyiṉukkut taviciṭṭu nāyi ṉēṟkē
  kāṭṭā taṉavellāṅ kāṭṭi.
eṉṟu aruḷic ceyvārāyiṉār.
itaṉkaṇ nāṭṭārkaḷ viḻittirukka eṉṟatu ulakattiluḷḷa makkaḷellārum iṟaivaṉaik kāṇṭaṟkum avaṉṟaṉ aruḷ utaviyaip peṟutaṟkum vēṭkai perituṭaiyarāy iṟaivaṉ tamakkumuṟ pulappaṭum nērattai etirpārttu atilē kaṇṇuṅ karuttumuṭaiyarāy muṉaintu niṟṟalai viḷakkā niṟkum. makkaḷuḷ ettuṇai iḻintōruṅ kaṭavuḷeṉṉuñ collaik kēṭṭa aḷavāṉē avaṉaik kāṇṭalil vēṭyuṅ karuttum uṭaiyarā yirukkiṉṟaṉar. avvāṟavar vēṭkai mikuntuḷḷārāyiṉum, av vēṭkaiyiṉpaṭi kaṭavuḷaik kāṇum muyaṟciyilēyē aḻunti niṟkamāṭṭāmal ulaka iṉpaṅkaḷil iḻuppuṇṭu pōtaliṉ, avar koṇṭa vēṭkai kaikūṭātu oḻikiṉṟatu. ivaraṅṅaṉa moḻiya, ip potu makkaḷiṉ vēṟāṉa cilar peruvēṭkaiyum perumuyaṟciyum uṭaiyarāyt tavamuyaṟciyiṟ pukuntu kaṭavuḷaik  kāṇṭalilēyē karuttu oruṅki niṟpar. aṅṅaṉam niṟpārkkuṅ kūṭa iṟaivaṉ kaṭpulaṉāyt tōṉṟi aruḷ ceyyātu, tamakku avaṉ nērē tōṉṟi aruḷ ceyta aruṭperun tiṟattai māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ ñālattuḷḷē nāyiṉukkut taviciṭṭu nāyiṉēṟkē, kāṭṭātaṉa vellāṅ kāṭṭi eṉṟu aruḷic ceyvārāyiṉar. iṅṅaṉamē piṟitōriṭattum,
marakatak kuvāal māmaṇip piṟakkam
 miṉṉoḷi koṇṭa poṉṉoḷi tikaḻat
 ticaimukaṉ ceṉṟu tēṭiṉark koḷittum
 muṟaiyuḷi oṟṟi muyaṉṟavark koḷittum
 oṟṟumai koṇṭu nōkkum uḷḷattu
 uṟṟavar varunta uṟaippavark koḷittum
 maṟaittiṟa nōkki varuntiṉark koḷittum
ittan tirattiṟ kāṇṭumeṉ ṟiruntōrkku
attan tirattiṉ avvayiṉ oḷittum
muṉivaṟa nōkki naṉivarak kauvi
āṇeṉat tēmāṉṟi aliyeṉap peyarntu
vāṇutaṟ peṇṇeṉa oḷittum cēṇvayiṉ
aimpulaṉ celaviṭuttu aruvarai toṟumpōyt
tuṟṟavai tuṟanta veṟṟuyi rākkai
aruntavar kāṭciyuḷ tirunta oḷittum
oṉṟuṇ ṭillai yeṉṟaṟivu oḷittum
paṇṭē payiltoṟum iṉṟē payiltoṟum
oḷikkuñ cōraṉaik kaṇṭaṉam,
ārmiṉ ārmiṉ nāṇmalarp piṇaiyaliṟ
ṟāṭaḷai iṭumiṉ
cuṟṟumiṉ cūḻmiṉ toṭarmiṉ viṭēṉmiṉ
paṟṟumiṉ eṉṟavar paṟṟumuṟ ṟoḷittum
taṉṉē rillōṉ tāṉēyāṉa taṉmai
eṉṉē raṉaiyōr kēṭkavan tiyampi
aṟaikūvi yāṭkoṇ ṭaruḷi
maṟaiyōr kōlaṅ kāṭṭi yaruḷalum   
(tiruvaṇṭappakuti 124 - 149)
eṉṟu aṭikaḷ tiruvāy malarntamai kāṇka.
aṟṟēl, tavamuyaṟciyiṟ pukunta muṉivararālum aṟiyappaṭāta mutalvaṉai at tavamuyaṟciyiṉṟi araca vāḻkkaiyilirunta māṇikkavācakar avaṉatu aruḷai nērē peṟṟaṉareṉṟalum, tammai nōkkit tavampurinta muṉivararkkum aruḷ vaḻaṅkātu am muyaṟcikkup puṟampāy niṉṟa aṭikaḷukku avvaruḷai vaḻaṅkiṉāṉeṉṟalum iṟaivaṉatu aruṭ ṭaṉmaikku iḻukkākātō eṉiṉ kūṟutum :
tava muyaṟciyaic ceyvārkaḷellān tāñ ceyyum  am muyaṟciyiṉālēyē tāṅkaḷ eṇṇiyavaikaḷai eṇṇiyapaṭiyē peṟutal kūṭumeṉṟum, piṟarai vāḻvittalun, tāḻvittalun tam collaḷaviṉālēyē nikaḻutal kūṭumeṉṟum, vēṟu em muyaṟcikkuṅ kiṭṭāta kaṭavuḷait tamatu tavamuyaṟciyiṉālēyē eḷitiṟ kiṭṭac ceytal kūṭumeṉṟum muṉaippuṭaṉ niṉaintu cerukkuṭai  yarāy niṟṟalāl, av varuntavattōr kāṭcikkuṅ kaṭavuḷ pulappaṭātavaṉāyt taṉṉai oḷippaṉ. teyvap pulamait tiruvaḷḷuvanāyaṉārum yāṉ eṉatu eṉṉuñ cerukkuṭaiyavarkaḷ ceyyun tavamuyaṟci kaḷellān tēvarkaḷuṟaiyun tuṟakkavulakaṅkaḷiṟ celuttu meṉṟum, avviruvakaic cerukkum aṟṟu muḻutum aṉpuruvāy niṉṟavarkaḷē vāṉōrkkum mēlāṉa iṟaivaṉṟaṉ pēriṉpa vulakappēṟṟiṉai yeytuvareṉṟum naṉku valiyuṟutti
yāṉeṉa teṉṉuñ cerukkaṟuppāṉ vāṉōrkku
  uyarnta ulakam pukum
eṉṟu aruḷic ceytaṉar. namatu ulakiya loḻukkat tilun tammaiyun tamataṟivaiyun tamatu muyaṟciyaiyumē periyaṉavākak karuti iṟumāntu niṟpavarkaḷ piṟarpāl aṉpuṭaiyarāy oḻukutalaik kāṇkilamaṉṟō? aṉpum cerukkum oḷiyum iruḷumpōlat tammil māṟupaṭṭa iyaṟkaiyiṉavākum. aṉpu nikaḻumuḷḷattil iṟumāppu uṇṭākamāṭṭātu; iṟumāppu mīteḻuntu niṟku muḷḷatti lō aṉpu ōreṭṭuṇaiyun talaikkāṭṭa māṭṭātu. ātalāl tavamuyaṟciyāṉatu mikavum pārāṭṭaṟpāla toṉṟē yāyiṉum, aḵtu ēṉai ulakiyal muyaṟcika ḷellāvaṟṟaiyum viṭa mikac ciṟantatē yāyiṉum atu nikaḻuṅkāl yāṉ eṉateṉ ṉuñ cerukkum ataṉōṭu uṭaṉ nikaḻutaliṉ ammuyaṟci yuṭaiya muṉivarkkum iṟaivaṉ pulappaṭṭut tōṉṟātu oḷippa ṉeṉṟār. ivvuṇmai āciriyar tirumūla nāyaṉārālum
eṉpē viṟakā iṟaicci yaṟuttiṭṭup
 poṉpōṟ kaṉaliṟ poriya vaṟuppiṉum
  aṉpō ṭuruki akaṅkuḻai vārkkaṉṟi
  eṉpoṉ maṇiyiṉai yeytavoṇ ṇātē
eṉṟu naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. piṟkālattirunta tāyumāṉac celvarum,
aruḷā levaiyumpā reṉṟāṉ-attai
	aṟiyātē cuṭṭiyeṉ ṉaṟivālē pārttēṉ
  iruḷāṉa poruḷkaṇṭa tallāṟ-kaṇṭa
	eṉṉaiyuṅ kaṇṭileṉ eṉṉēṭi tōḻi.
eṉṟaruḷic ceytār. yāṉeṉa teṉṉun talaiyeṭup puṭaiyavarkaḷukku ulakattavar nēcattaip peṟutalum iyalātirukkaiyil, ataic ciṟitēṉum uṭaiyavarkaḷ avviruḷukkuḷ maṟainta oḷipōṟ pulappaṭātu niṟkum iṟaivaṉai yāṅṅaṉam kāṇpar? aṉpiṉāl akaṅkarai vāriṭattu ettakaiya iṟumāppum illāmaiyiṉ, avarpāl ellārum aṉpu nikaḻap peṟukiṉṟaṉar. innikaḻcciyō ṭoppavē ellām valla iṟaivaṉum aṉpuṭaiyārkku maṭṭumē aṇukkaṉāy niṉṟu aruḷ puriyum nīrmaiyaṉāvaṉ. ivvāṟṟāl, aṉpunilai yoṉṟē tavanilaiyiṉum mēṟ paṭṭatākum. aṉpunilaiyoṉṟē tavanilaikku meṭ ṭāta mutalvaṉait taṉṉakap paṭuttu vatākum. tavanilaiyō muyaṟci yuṭaiyatu. aṉpunilaiyō muyaṟciyaṟṟatu muyaṟci yuṭaiyār muyaṉṟa poruḷaip peṟuvatu iyalpāyirukkat tavamuyaṟci yuṭaiyār kaṭavuḷai yeytāmaiyum attakaiya muyaṟci yillāta aṉparāyiṉār āṇṭavaṉaik kāṇṭalum viyakkaṟ pālaṉavā mallavō? āyiṉum muyaṟciyāṟ peṟappaṭu vaṉa vellām kālatti ṉellaiyilum iṭattiṉellai yilum akappaṭṭa varam puṭaip poruḷkaḷāy aḻiyun taṉmaiya vāy iruttalāl, kālattaik kaṭantum iṭattaik kaṭantum, aḻiviṉṟiyum, māṟutaliṉṟiyum varampilaṉāy viḷaṅkāniṉṟa mutalvaṉai attakaiya muyaṟcikaḷ ettuṇaic ciṟantaṉavā yiruppiṉum avai yāṅṅaṉañ ceṉṟu paṟṟum? maṟṟu, aṉpō attakaiya muyaṟciyiṉ pāṟ paṭivatiṉṟik kālat tāṉum iṭattāṉum varaiyaṟukkap paṭātatāy aḻiviṉṟi māṟiṉṟi eṉṟum orupaṭittāy niṟaintu niṟkumvaḻi, annilaiyil taṉṉōṭotta iṟaivaṉai atu tāṉ peṟṟu iṉpuṟutalum iyaṟkai yākum. ip peṟṟittākiya pēraṉ pāl viḻuṅkap peṟṟavar evvakaic ceyalumiṉṟi evvakai muyaṟciyumiṉṟip peruñ cōmparāyk kiṭappiṉum avarē iṟaivaṉṟaṉ aruṭperu viḷakkaṅkaḷellām eḷitiṟ kāṇpar eṉpatu terippār,
cōmpar iruppatu cutta veḷiyilē
  cōmpar kiṭappatu cutta veḷiyilē
  cōmpar uṇarvu curuti muṭintiṭam
  cōmpar kaṇṭārac curutikkaṭ ṭūkkamē
eṉṟum,
tūṅkik kaṇṭārciva lōkamun tammuḷḷē
 tūṅkik kāṇṭār civa yōkamun tammuḷḷē
 tūṅkik kaṇṭārciva pōkamun tammuḷḷē
 tūṅkik kaṇṭārnilai colva tevvāṟē.
